You are building a procedural audio synthesizer for a music project. Work in /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/synth/ (create it). The repo root /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop is a git repo; commit your work as you reach testable states (normal commits, no amending or rebasing, end commit messages with the line Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>). Follow the user's global rules: never cd; run everything with absolute paths from the repo root; run slow things in the background.

The song

"It's 2am and I'm Crying" is a club track sung from the point of view of a one-year-old baby. The hook lines, transcribed from the baby's parent, are:

It's 2am and I'm crying
It's 3am and I'm crying
It's 11am and I'm napping

The genre is "untz untz untz": four-on-the-floor Eurodance / big-room house, 128 BPM, 4/4, a sidechain-pumping supersaw pad, offbeat open hi-hats, a clap on 2 and 4, a sub bass that locks to the kick, a bright supersaw hook lead, white-noise risers and reverse-cymbal swells into each drop, and a cheeky pitched-down "untz" vocal-like kick texture. Think 2009-2012 Eurodance (Cascada, Basshunter) collided with hyperpop brightness. The key is A minor (relative to a hopeful C major lift in the final chorus is a nice option). The lyric mood is deadpan: a baby stating facts about its night at full club intensity.

One signature idea to try: the hook "vocal" in this instrumental demo is a formant-synthesized baby cry, a saw/pulse oscillator with vibrato through 3 to 4 resonant bandpass filters sweeping between vowel formants (the "waaaah" vowel is roughly /a/ to /ɛ/, F1 around 700 to 900 Hz, F2 around 1200 to 1800 Hz for an infant, with a high fundamental around 400 to 500 Hz for a real cry; for a melodic lead you can transpose the fundamental to the melody notes and keep the formants). Make it sit as a lead instrument playing the hook melody, not as a literal recording. If it sounds bad after a few iterations, fall back to a plain supersaw lead and note why.

Hook melody scansion

"it's-TWO-a-M-and-I'm-CRY-ing": 8 syllables. Suggested placement in one 4/4 bar at 128 BPM with "it's" as a pickup on the "and" of 4 of the previous bar: TWO on beat 1, a-M on the "and" of 1 and beat 2, and-I'm on the "and" of 2 and beat 3, CRY on beat 4 (held), -ing on the "and" of 4. The three hook lines cycle over a four-bar phrase with the fourth bar either a rest or a fill. "e-LEV-en-a-M-and-I'm-NAP-ping" has two more syllables, so it compresses into sixteenths at the start of its bar; that rhythmic stumble is part of the joke. Compose a simple, singable minor-pentatonic-leaning melody for this and write it down in the spec.

Deliverables

  1. A pyproject.toml managed by uv (Python 3.12 or newer; numpy, scipy, soundfile; optionally matplotlib for spectrograms). No other heavy deps. Run with uv run --project /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/synth ....
  2. song.py (or a small package): a declarative song spec (sections with bar counts, chord progression per section, drum patterns as step strings, melody as note/duration lists) plus a renderer that turns the spec into a stereo 44.1 kHz 24-bit WAV. Target structure around 2:45 to 3:15: intro (4 bars, filtered, hats only) → verse 1 (16 bars, kick + bass + sparse pad) → pre-chorus / build (8 bars, riser, snare roll) → chorus/drop (16 bars, full) → verse 2 (16) → build (8) → chorus (16) → bridge "11am and I'm napping" (8 bars, half-time feel, music-box / celesta lullaby voicing of the hook, filtered, no kick; then a riser) → final chorus (16, add a top octave, maybe the C-major lift) → outro (4 to 8 bars, drums drop out, pad tail). Every section boundary must fall on a bar line.
  3. A sidechain compressor keyed from the kick (classic pumping), a simple reverb (feedback delay network or convolution with a synthetic impulse response), a master limiter so peaks sit at -1 dBTP and integrated loudness lands around -9 to -11 LUFS (implement a basic K-weighted loudness measurement; exactness is not needed).
  4. Tight iteration loop: write analyze.py that prints for a rendered WAV: duration, peak dBFS, approximate integrated LUFS, crest factor, per-band energy (sub <60 Hz, bass 60-250, low-mid 250-2k, high-mid 2k-6k, air >6k) in dB relative to full-scale, a clipping count, and saves a spectrogram PNG. Then iterate: render → analyze → look at the spectrogram PNG with the Read tool → adjust the spec or synth → repeat. Do at least 5 full iterations and log each one in ITERATIONS.md (what you changed, what the numbers said, what you heard in the spectrogram). Render time for the full song should stay under about 60 seconds; vectorize with numpy, no per-sample Python loops except inside small filters where unavoidable (use scipy.signal.lfilter).
  5. song.json written alongside the WAV: bpm, bar length in seconds, the list of sections with start/end seconds and bar counts, the beat grid (list of all beat times), downbeats, and the hook melody note events with onset times. A video renderer will read this to sync visuals, so it must match the audio exactly.
  6. Also render 10-second previews of the chorus and the bridge as separate WAVs so a human can audition quickly.
  7. A README.md with run commands and a short description of the signal chain.

Put rendered audio in /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/synth/out/ (gitignored by the repo's *.wav rule; commit the code, spec, JSON, PNGs and ITERATIONS.md, not the WAVs).

Work independently and finish all deliverables. Report back with: the final analyze.py numbers, the section table from song.json, whether the formant baby-cry lead worked, and the exact commands to re-render and to play the previews (afplay is available on macOS).

BashWrite loudness module and run sanity check

description Write loudness module and run sanity check
cat > /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/synth/loudness.py <<'EOF'
"""Loudness and peak measurement shared by the master limiter and analyze.py.

Approximate ITU-R BS.1770-4: K-weighting (high shelf + RLB high-pass) with the
biquads re-derived for the actual sample rate (the standard's published
coefficients are for 48 kHz), 400 ms blocks with 75 % overlap, absolute gate
at -70 LUFS and relative gate at -10 LU. True peak uses 4x polyphase
oversampling.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
from scipy import signal

# Analog prototype parameters behind the BS.1770 48 kHz coefficients
# (as reverse-engineered in pyloudnorm / libebur128).
_SHELF_GAIN_DB = 3.999843853973347
_SHELF_F0 = 1681.974450955533
_SHELF_Q = 0.7071752369554196
_HP_F0 = 38.13547087602444
_HP_Q = 0.5003270373238773


def k_weighting_sos(sr: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Return the two K-weighting biquads as an SOS array for sample rate sr."""
    # Stage 1: high shelf (head acoustics)
    A = 10 ** (_SHELF_GAIN_DB / 40.0)
    w0 = 2 * np.pi * _SHELF_F0 / sr
    alpha = np.sin(w0) / (2 * _SHELF_Q)
    cw = np.cos(w0)
    sA = np.sqrt(A)
    b0 = A * ((A + 1) + (A - 1) * cw + 2 * sA * alpha)
    b1 = -2 * A * ((A - 1) + (A + 1) * cw)
    b2 = A * ((A + 1) + (A - 1) * cw - 2 * sA * alpha)
    a0 = (A + 1) - (A - 1) * cw + 2 * sA * alpha
    a1 = 2 * ((A - 1) - (A + 1) * cw)
    a2 = (A + 1) - (A - 1) * cw - 2 * sA * alpha
    shelf = np.array([b0, b1, b2, a0, a1, a2]) / a0
    # Stage 2: RLB high-pass
    w0 = 2 * np.pi * _HP_F0 / sr
    alpha = np.sin(w0) / (2 * _HP_Q)
    cw = np.cos(w0)
    b0 = (1 + cw) / 2
    b1 = -(1 + cw)
    b2 = (1 + cw) / 2
    a0 = 1 + alpha
    a1 = -2 * cw
    a2 = 1 - alpha
    hp = np.array([b0, b1, b2, a0, a1, a2]) / a0
    return np.vstack([shelf, hp])


def _as_2d(x: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    return x[:, None] if x.ndim == 1 else x


def k_weighted_power(x: np.ndarray, sr: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Per-sample K-weighted power summed over channels (all weights 1.0)."""
    x = _as_2d(x)
    y = signal.sosfilt(k_weighting_sos(sr), x, axis=0)
    return np.sum(y * y, axis=1)


def _block_loudness(power: np.ndarray, sr: int, block_s=0.4, overlap=0.75):
    n = int(round(block_s * sr))
    hop = int(round(n * (1 - overlap)))
    if len(power) < n:
        return np.array([np.mean(power)]) if len(power) else np.array([0.0])
    c = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(power)])
    starts = np.arange(0, len(power) - n + 1, hop)
    return (c[starts + n] - c[starts]) / n


def integrated_lufs(x: np.ndarray, sr: int) -> float:
    """Gated integrated loudness in LUFS (approximate BS.1770-4)."""
    z = _block_loudness(k_weighted_power(x, sr), sr)
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore"):
        lk = -0.691 + 10 * np.log10(np.maximum(z, 1e-20))
    keep = lk > -70.0
    if not np.any(keep):
        return -np.inf
    rel = -0.691 + 10 * np.log10(np.mean(z[keep])) - 10.0
    keep &= lk > rel
    if not np.any(keep):
        return -np.inf
    return float(-0.691 + 10 * np.log10(np.mean(z[keep])))


def ungated_lufs(power_slice: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """Loudness of a span from precomputed K-weighted power (no gating)."""
    m = float(np.mean(power_slice)) if len(power_slice) else 0.0
    return -0.691 + 10 * np.log10(max(m, 1e-20))


def true_peak_envelope(x: np.ndarray, oversample: int = 4) -> np.ndarray:
    """Per-sample max |x| over channels and the oversampled points in [n, n+1)."""
    x = _as_2d(x)
    up = signal.resample_poly(x, oversample, 1, axis=0)
    a = np.max(np.abs(up), axis=1)
    a = a[: x.shape[0] * oversample].reshape(x.shape[0], oversample)
    return np.maximum(a.max(axis=1), np.max(np.abs(x), axis=1))


def true_peak_dbtp(x: np.ndarray, oversample: int = 4) -> float:
    return float(20 * np.log10(max(true_peak_envelope(x, oversample).max(), 1e-12)))


def sample_peak_dbfs(x: np.ndarray) -> float:
    return float(20 * np.log10(max(np.max(np.abs(x)), 1e-12)))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Sanity check: 997 Hz sine at -20 dBFS on both channels reads ~ -20 LUFS.
    sr = 44100
    t = np.arange(sr * 10) / sr
    s = 0.1 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 997 * t)
    st = np.stack([s, s], axis=1)
    print(f"997 Hz -20 dBFS stereo: {integrated_lufs(st, sr):.2f} LUFS (expect ~ -20.0)")
    print(f"same, one channel only: {integrated_lufs(np.stack([s, 0*s], 1), sr):.2f} LUFS (expect ~ -23.0)")
    print(f"true peak of 0.1 sine: {true_peak_dbtp(st):.2f} dBTP (expect ~ -20.0)")
EOF

import re
p='/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/synth/loudness.py'
src=open(p).read()
start=src.index('def k_weighting_sos')
end=src.index('def _as_2d')
new='''def k_weighting_sos(sr: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Return the two K-weighting biquads as an SOS array for sample rate sr.

    Uses the libebur128 bilinear formulation, which reproduces the published
    48 kHz BS.1770 coefficients exactly and generalises to other rates.
    """
    # Stage 1: high shelf (head acoustics)
    K = np.tan(np.pi * _SHELF_F0 / sr)
    Vh = 10 ** (_SHELF_GAIN_DB / 20.0)
    Vb = Vh ** 0.4996667741545416
    a0 = 1.0 + K / _SHELF_Q + K * K
    shelf = np.array([
        (Vh + Vb * K / _SHELF_Q + K * K) / a0,
        2.0 * (K * K - Vh) / a0,
        (Vh - Vb * K / _SHELF_Q + K * K) / a0,
        1.0,
        2.0 * (K * K - 1.0) / a0,
        (1.0 - K / _SHELF_Q + K * K) / a0,
    ])
    # Stage 2: RLB high-pass
    K = np.tan(np.pi * _HP_F0 / sr)
    a0 = 1.0 + K / _HP_Q + K * K
    hp = np.array([1.0, -2.0, 1.0, 1.0, 2.0 * (K * K - 1.0) / a0, (1.0 - K / _HP_Q + K * K) / a0])
    return np.vstack([shelf, hp])


'''
src=src[:start]+new+src[end:]
open(p,'w').write(src)
EOF

"""Vectorised DSP primitives: oscillators, envelopes, filters, mixing helpers.

Everything works on whole numpy arrays. The only Python loops are over
blocks of a few hundred samples inside time-varying filters, where each block
is still filtered by scipy.signal.lfilter with carried state.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
from scipy import signal

SR = 44100
RNG = np.random.default_rng(20260202)


def rng(seed: int) -> np.random.Generator:
    return np.random.default_rng(seed)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- pitch ----
NOTE_PC = {"C": 0, "D": 2, "E": 4, "F": 5, "G": 7, "A": 9, "B": 11}


def note_to_midi(name: str) -> int:
    """'A4' -> 69, 'C#5' -> 73, 'Bb3' -> 58."""
    letter = name[0].upper()
    rest = name[1:]
    acc = 0
    while rest and rest[0] in "#b":
        acc += 1 if rest[0] == "#" else -1
        rest = rest[1:]
    octave = int(rest)
    return 12 * (octave + 1) + NOTE_PC[letter] + acc


def midi_to_hz(m):
    return 440.0 * 2.0 ** ((np.asarray(m, dtype=np.float64) - 69.0) / 12.0)


def cents(c):
    return 2.0 ** (np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64) / 1200.0)


# ----------------------------------------------------------- oscillators ----
def _polyblep(t: np.ndarray, dt: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    out = np.zeros_like(t)
    m = t < dt
    x = t[m] / dt[m] if np.ndim(dt) else t[m] / dt
    out[m] = x + x - x * x - 1.0
    m2 = t > 1.0 - dt
    x = (t[m2] - 1.0) / (dt[m2] if np.ndim(dt) else dt)
    out[m2] = x * x + x + x + 1.0
    return out


def phase_from_freq(freq, n: int, phase0: float = 0.0) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Return (phase in [0,1), per-sample increment) for a frequency (scalar or array)."""
    dt = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(freq, dtype=np.float64) / SR, (n,)).copy()
    ph = phase0 + np.cumsum(dt) - dt
    return ph - np.floor(ph), dt


def saw(freq, n: int, phase0: float = 0.0) -> np.ndarray:
    """Band-limited (polyBLEP) sawtooth in [-1, 1]."""
    ph, dt = phase_from_freq(freq, n, phase0)
    return 2.0 * ph - 1.0 - _polyblep(ph, dt)


def pulse(freq, n: int, duty: float = 0.5, phase0: float = 0.0) -> np.ndarray:
    """Band-limited pulse built from two polyBLEP saws."""
    ph, dt = phase_from_freq(freq, n, phase0)
    ph2 = ph + duty
    ph2 -= np.floor(ph2)
    s1 = 2.0 * ph - 1.0 - _polyblep(ph, dt)
    s2 = 2.0 * ph2 - 1.0 - _polyblep(ph2, dt)
    return s1 - s2 + (2.0 * duty - 1.0)


def sine(freq, n: int, phase0: float = 0.0) -> np.ndarray:
    ph, _ = phase_from_freq(freq, n, phase0)
    return np.sin(2 * np.pi * ph)


def noise(n: int, seed: int = 0) -> np.ndarray:
    return rng(seed).standard_normal(n)


# ------------------------------------------------------------- envelopes ----
def env_points(n: int, points: list[tuple[float, float]]) -> np.ndarray:
    """Piecewise-linear envelope from (time_s, level) points over n samples."""
    t = np.arange(n) / SR
    ts, vs = zip(*points)
    return np.interp(t, ts, vs)


def adsr(n_gate: int, n_total: int, a=0.005, d=0.1, s=0.7, r=0.1, curve=True) -> np.ndarray:
    """ADSR over n_total samples with the gate open for n_gate samples."""
    t = np.arange(n_total) / SR
    gate_t = n_gate / SR
    env = np.empty(n_total)
    att = t < a
    env[att] = t[att] / a if a > 0 else 1.0
    dec = (~att) & (t < gate_t)
    td = t[dec] - a
    env[dec] = s + (1 - s) * np.exp(-td / max(d, 1e-4)) if curve else np.maximum(s, 1 - td / d * (1 - s))
    # level at gate close (handles gates shorter than attack)
    if gate_t < a:
        lvl = gate_t / a
    else:
        lvl = s + (1 - s) * np.exp(-(gate_t - a) / max(d, 1e-4))
    rel = t >= gate_t
    env[rel] = lvl * np.exp(-(t[rel] - gate_t) / max(r, 1e-4) * 3.0) if curve else lvl * np.maximum(0, 1 - (t[rel] - gate_t) / r)
    return env


def exp_decay(n: int, tau: float) -> np.ndarray:
    return np.exp(-np.arange(n) / (SR * tau))


def fade(x: np.ndarray, fin: float = 0.002, fout: float = 0.005) -> np.ndarray:
    """Short linear fades to kill clicks at segment edges (in place, returns x)."""
    n = x.shape[0]
    a = min(int(fin * SR), n // 2)
    b = min(int(fout * SR), n // 2)
    if a > 0:
        ramp = np.linspace(0, 1, a)
        x[:a] *= ramp if x.ndim == 1 else ramp[:, None]
    if b > 0:
        ramp = np.linspace(1, 0, b)
        x[n - b:] *= ramp if x.ndim == 1 else ramp[:, None]
    return x


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- biquads ---
def biquad_coeffs(kind: str, fc, q, gain_db=0.0):
    """RBJ cookbook biquad coefficients; fc and q may be arrays (vectorised).

    Returns (b, a) with shapes (..., 3)."""
    fc = np.clip(np.asarray(fc, dtype=np.float64), 10.0, SR * 0.49)
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    w0 = 2 * np.pi * fc / SR
    cw, sw = np.cos(w0), np.sin(w0)
    alpha = sw / (2 * q)
    one = np.ones_like(w0)
    if kind == "lp":
        b = np.stack([(1 - cw) / 2, 1 - cw, (1 - cw) / 2], -1)
        a = np.stack([1 + alpha, -2 * cw, 1 - alpha], -1)
    elif kind == "hp":
        b = np.stack([(1 + cw) / 2, -(1 + cw), (1 + cw) / 2], -1)
        a = np.stack([1 + alpha, -2 * cw, 1 - alpha], -1)
    elif kind == "bp":  # constant 0 dB peak gain
        b = np.stack([alpha, 0 * one, -alpha], -1)
        a = np.stack([1 + alpha, -2 * cw, 1 - alpha], -1)
    elif kind == "peak":
        A = 10 ** (np.asarray(gain_db) / 40)
        b = np.stack([1 + alpha * A, -2 * cw, 1 - alpha * A], -1)
        a = np.stack([1 + alpha / A, -2 * cw, 1 - alpha / A], -1)
    else:
        raise ValueError(kind)
    return b / a[..., :1], a / a[..., :1]


def biquad(x: np.ndarray, kind: str, fc: float, q: float = 0.707, gain_db=0.0) -> np.ndarray:
    b, a = biquad_coeffs(kind, fc, q, gain_db)
    return signal.lfilter(b, a, x, axis=0)


def butter(x: np.ndarray, kind: str, fc, order: int = 2) -> np.ndarray:
    sos = signal.butter(order, fc, btype={"lp": "lowpass", "hp": "highpass", "bp": "bandpass"}[kind],
                        fs=SR, output="sos")
    return signal.sosfilt(sos, x, axis=0)


def tv_biquad(x: np.ndarray, kind: str, fc, q, block: int = 128, gain_db=0.0) -> np.ndarray:
    """Time-varying biquad: coefficients recomputed per block, state carried.

    fc and q may be scalars or per-sample arrays (sampled at block centres)."""
    n = x.shape[0]
    nb = (n + block - 1) // block
    centres = np.minimum(np.arange(nb) * block + block // 2, n - 1)
    fcb = np.asarray(fc)[centres] if np.ndim(fc) else np.full(nb, float(fc))
    qb = np.asarray(q)[centres] if np.ndim(q) else np.full(nb, float(q))
    gb = np.asarray(gain_db)[centres] if np.ndim(gain_db) else np.full(nb, float(gain_db))
    B, A = biquad_coeffs(kind, fcb, qb, gb)
    y = np.empty_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
    zshape = (2,) + x.shape[1:]
    zi = np.zeros(zshape)
    lf = signal.lfilter
    for i in range(nb):
        s = i * block
        e = min(s + block, n)
        y[s:e], zi = lf(B[i], A[i], x[s:e], axis=0, zi=zi)
    return y


def one_pole_lp(x: np.ndarray, tau_s: float) -> np.ndarray:
    a = np.exp(-1.0 / (SR * tau_s))
    return signal.lfilter([1 - a], [1, -a], x, axis=0)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- mixing ---
def pan_gains(p: float) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """Equal-power pan, p in [-1, 1]."""
    th = (p + 1) * np.pi / 4
    return float(np.cos(th)), float(np.sin(th))


def to_stereo(x: np.ndarray, pan: float = 0.0) -> np.ndarray:
    if x.ndim == 2:
        return x
    l, r = pan_gains(pan)
    return np.stack([x * l, x * r], axis=1)


def add_at(buf: np.ndarray, start: int, sig: np.ndarray, gain: float = 1.0) -> None:
    """Mix sig into buf at sample start (clipped to buffer bounds)."""
    n = buf.shape[0]
    if start >= n:
        return
    s0 = max(start, 0)
    off = s0 - start
    e = min(start + sig.shape[0], n)
    if e <= s0:
        return
    seg = sig[off: off + (e - s0)]
    if buf.ndim == 2 and seg.ndim == 1:
        buf[s0:e] += gain * seg[:, None] * np.sqrt(0.5)
    else:
        buf[s0:e] += gain * seg


def db(x):
    return 20 * np.log10(np.maximum(np.abs(x), 1e-12))


def undb(d):
    return 10 ** (np.asarray(d) / 20)


def normalize(x: np.ndarray, peak: float = 1.0) -> np.ndarray:
    m = np.max(np.abs(x))
    return x * (peak / m) if m > 0 else x

"""Effects: envelope followers, kick-keyed sidechain compressor, convolution
reverb with a synthetic impulse response, ping-pong delay."""
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
from scipy import signal

from dsp import SR, butter, db, one_pole_lp, rng


def peak_release(x: np.ndarray, tau_samples: float) -> np.ndarray:
    """y[n] = max_{k<=n} x[k] * exp(-(n-k)/tau): instant attack, exponential release.

    Vectorised exactly via a running maximum in the log domain:
    log y[n] = max_k (log x[k] + k/tau) - n/tau.
    """
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.arange(x.shape[0]) / float(tau_samples)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(x, 1e-30)) + idx
    return np.exp(np.maximum.accumulate(z) - idx)


def sidechain_gain_reduction(key: np.ndarray, threshold_db: float = -24.0, ratio: float = 10.0,
                             knee_db: float = 6.0, attack_ms: float = 1.5,
                             release_ms: float = 160.0) -> np.ndarray:
    """Compressor gain computer driven by a key signal (the kick bus).

    Returns gain reduction in dB (>= 0) per sample. Detector: peak follower
    with 20 ms release; static curve: soft-knee threshold/ratio; ballistics:
    instant-attack/exponential-release followed by a short attack smoother."""
    k = np.abs(key if key.ndim == 1 else key.mean(axis=1))
    env = peak_release(k, 0.020 * SR)
    over = db(env) - threshold_db
    slope = 1.0 - 1.0 / ratio
    gr = np.where(over > knee_db / 2, over * slope, 0.0)
    kn = np.abs(over) <= knee_db / 2
    gr[kn] = slope * (over[kn] + knee_db / 2) ** 2 / (2 * knee_db)
    gr = peak_release(gr, release_ms / 1000 * SR)
    return one_pole_lp(gr, attack_ms / 1000)


def apply_gr(x: np.ndarray, gr_db: np.ndarray, depth: float) -> np.ndarray:
    g = 10 ** (-depth * gr_db / 20)
    return x * (g[:, None] if x.ndim == 2 else g)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- reverb ----
def make_ir(seconds: float = 2.8, rt60=(2.6, 2.0, 1.1), predelay: float = 0.02,
            seed: int = 7) -> np.ndarray:
    """Synthetic stereo hall IR: band-split decaying noise (low/mid/high RT60s),
    a diffusion fade-in, a handful of early reflections, energy-normalised."""
    n = int(seconds * SR)
    t = np.arange(n) / SR
    g = rng(seed)
    ir = np.zeros((n, 2))
    for ch in range(2):
        w = g.standard_normal(n)
        lo = butter(w, "lp", 450.0, 2)
        hi = butter(w, "hp", 4500.0, 2)
        mid = w - lo - hi
        bands = [lo, mid, hi]
        tail = sum(b * np.exp(-6.91 * t / rt) for b, rt in zip(bands, rt60))
        tail *= 1.0 - np.exp(-t / 0.015)
        # early reflections (different per channel for width)
        er = np.zeros(n)
        for tap_ms, amp in zip(g.uniform(7, 60, 9), g.uniform(0.3, 0.8, 9)):
            er[int(tap_ms / 1000 * SR)] += amp * (1 if g.random() > 0.5 else -1)
        er = butter(er, "lp", 7000.0, 2)
        ir[:, ch] = tail / np.sqrt(np.sum(tail ** 2)) + 0.35 * er / max(np.sqrt(np.sum(er ** 2)), 1e-9)
    pd = int(predelay * SR)
    ir = np.concatenate([np.zeros((pd, 2)), ir])[:n]
    return ir / np.sqrt(np.mean(np.sum(ir ** 2, axis=0)))


def convolve_reverb(x: np.ndarray, ir: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Stereo-in, stereo-out convolution; output trimmed to len(x) (no latency)."""
    n = x.shape[0]
    out = np.empty((n, 2))
    for ch in range(2):
        out[:, ch] = signal.oaconvolve(x[:, ch], ir[:, ch], mode="full")[:n]
    return out


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- delay ----
def pingpong(x: np.ndarray, delay_s: float, feedback: float = 0.45, repeats: int = 6,
             lp_hz: float = 5000.0, hp_hz: float = 300.0) -> np.ndarray:
    """Finite ping-pong echo train (vectorised): odd repeats left, even right,
    each repeat darker by one pass of a low-pass."""
    mono = x.mean(axis=1) if x.ndim == 2 else x
    n = mono.shape[0]
    d = int(round(delay_s * SR))
    out = np.zeros((n, 2))
    cur = butter(mono, "hp", hp_hz, 2)
    for k in range(1, repeats + 1):
        cur = butter(cur, "lp", lp_hz, 1) * (feedback if k > 1 else 1.0)
        sh = k * d
        if sh >= n:
            break
        out[sh:, (k - 1) % 2] += cur[: n - sh]
    return out

"""Master bus: DC/sub-rumble high-pass, soft clipper, true-peak look-ahead
limiter and an auto-gain loop that lands integrated loudness on target."""
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
from scipy.ndimage import maximum_filter1d, uniform_filter1d

from dsp import SR, butter, db, undb
from fx import peak_release
from loudness import integrated_lufs, true_peak_dbtp, true_peak_envelope


def soft_clip(x: np.ndarray, threshold: float = 0.75, ceiling: float = 1.0) -> np.ndarray:
    """Identity below threshold, tanh knee that saturates at ceiling."""
    a = np.abs(x)
    over = a > threshold
    k = ceiling - threshold
    y = x.copy()
    y[over] = np.sign(x[over]) * (threshold + k * np.tanh((a[over] - threshold) / k))
    return y


def limiter(x: np.ndarray, ceiling_dbtp: float = -1.2, lookahead_ms: float = 1.5,
            release_ms: float = 70.0, oversample: int = 4) -> np.ndarray:
    """Zero-latency (offline look-ahead) brickwall limiter on the true-peak envelope.

    Required gain reduction is held over +-L samples (max filter), released
    exponentially, then smoothed by a centred (2L+1) moving average. Because
    every sample in the averaging window was held at >= the requirement at n,
    the smoothed reduction never undershoots, so peaks stay under the ceiling
    without delaying the audio (the beat grid in song.json stays exact)."""
    p = true_peak_envelope(x, oversample)
    req = np.maximum(0.0, db(p) - ceiling_dbtp)
    L = max(1, int(lookahead_ms / 1000 * SR))
    h = maximum_filter1d(req, size=2 * L + 1, mode="nearest")
    h = peak_release(h, release_ms / 1000 * SR)
    s = uniform_filter1d(h, size=2 * L + 1, mode="nearest")
    return x * undb(-s)[:, None]


def master_chain(mix: np.ndarray, target_lufs: float = -10.0, ceiling_dbtp: float = -1.2,
                 clip_threshold: float = 0.72, max_iter: int = 5, verbose: bool = True):
    mix = butter(mix, "hp", 24.0, 2)
    g = target_lufs - integrated_lufs(mix)
    hist = []
    y = mix
    for it in range(max_iter):
        pre = mix * undb(g)
        # the clipper works relative to the limiter ceiling
        c = undb(ceiling_dbtp)
        y = soft_clip(pre / c, clip_threshold, 1.0) * c
        y = limiter(y, ceiling_dbtp)
        if true_peak_dbtp(y) > ceiling_dbtp + 0.05:  # second safety pass
            y = limiter(y, ceiling_dbtp)
        L = integrated_lufs(y)
        hist.append((round(float(g), 2), round(L, 2)))
        if verbose:
            print(f"  master pass {it}: pre-gain {g:+.2f} dB -> {L:.2f} LUFS, TP {true_peak_dbtp(y):.2f} dBTP")
        if abs(L - target_lufs) < 0.15:
            break
        if len(hist) >= 2 and hist[-1][1] != hist[-2][1]:
            slope = (hist[-1][1] - hist[-2][1]) / (hist[-1][0] - hist[-2][0])
            slope = float(np.clip(slope, 0.2, 1.0))
        else:
            slope = 0.7
        g += (target_lufs - L) / slope
    return y, {"pre_gain_db": float(g), "passes": hist}

"""Instruments. One-shots (drums, untz) are rendered once and pasted; tonal
voices are rendered per note or per phrase with vectorised numpy."""
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from dsp import (SR, adsr, biquad, butter, cents, exp_decay, fade, midi_to_hz, noise,
                 one_pole_lp, pulse, rng, saw, sine, tv_biquad)


# =================================================================== drums ==
def kick(end_hz: float = 55.0) -> np.ndarray:
    """Four-on-the-floor kick tuned to A1: pitch-swept sine + click, saturated."""
    n = int(0.42 * SR)
    t = np.arange(n) / SR
    f = end_hz + 210 * np.exp(-t / 0.014) + 45 * np.exp(-t / 0.07)
    body = np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(f) / SR)
    amp = np.where(t < 0.05, 1.0, np.exp(-(t - 0.05) / 0.13))
    click = butter(noise(n, 11), "hp", 2500) * np.exp(-t / 0.0025) * 0.35
    k = body * amp + click
    k = np.tanh(1.8 * k) / np.tanh(1.8)
    return fade(k, 0.0, 0.01)


def clap() -> np.ndarray:
    """Stereo clap: three fast noise bursts + tail, band-passed around 1.2 kHz."""
    n = int(0.4 * SR)
    t = np.arange(n) / SR
    env = np.zeros(n)
    for i, t0 in enumerate([0.0, 0.009, 0.019]):
        m = t >= t0
        env[m] += np.exp(-(t[m] - t0) / 0.0035) * (0.8 + 0.1 * i)
    m = t >= 0.026
    env[m] += 0.75 * np.exp(-(t[m] - 0.026) / 0.09)
    out = np.zeros((n, 2))
    for ch in range(2):
        w = noise(n, 21 + ch)
        b = biquad(w, "bp", 1250, 0.8) + 0.35 * butter(w, "hp", 3500)
        out[:, ch] = b * env
    return fade(out / np.max(np.abs(out)), 0.0, 0.02)


def _metal(n: int, base: float = 1.9) -> np.ndarray:
    """808-style metallic cluster: six band-limited squares at inharmonic ratios."""
    fs = np.array([205.3, 304.4, 369.6, 522.7, 540.0, 800.0]) * base
    return sum(pulse(f, n, 0.5, phase0=i * 0.17) for i, f in enumerate(fs)) / 6


def hat(decay: float, seed: int = 31) -> np.ndarray:
    n = int(min(6 * decay + 0.02, 1.0) * SR)
    t = np.arange(n) / SR
    m = _metal(n)
    w = noise(n, seed)
    x = 0.6 * biquad(m, "bp", 9500, 1.2) + 0.5 * butter(w, "hp", 8000, 2)
    x = butter(x, "hp", 6500, 2)
    env = np.exp(-t / decay) * (1 - np.exp(-t / 0.0006))
    return fade(x * env / np.max(np.abs(x * env)), 0.0, 0.005)


def snare() -> np.ndarray:
    n = int(0.25 * SR)
    t = np.arange(n) / SR
    tone = np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(185 + 60 * np.exp(-t / 0.01)) / SR) * np.exp(-t / 0.05)
    w = noise(n, 41)
    nz = (biquad(w, "bp", 3200, 0.7) + 0.4 * butter(w, "hp", 6000)) * np.exp(-t / 0.075)
    x = 0.55 * tone + nz
    return fade(x / np.max(np.abs(x)), 0.0, 0.01)


def crash(seconds: float = 2.6, seed: int = 51) -> np.ndarray:
    n = int(seconds * SR)
    t = np.arange(n) / SR
    out = np.zeros((n, 2))
    for ch in range(2):
        w = noise(n, seed + ch)
        x = butter(w, "hp", 4200, 2) + 0.3 * biquad(_metal(n, 2.6 + 0.07 * ch), "bp", 7000, 0.7)
        out[:, ch] = x * np.exp(-t / 0.55) * (1 - np.exp(-t / 0.002))
    return fade(out / np.max(np.abs(out)), 0.0, 0.05)


def reverse_cymbal(seconds: float, seed: int = 61) -> np.ndarray:
    """Reversed crash, so it swells into the downbeat it ends on."""
    c = crash(max(seconds, 0.5), seed)[: int(seconds * SR)]
    c = c[::-1].copy()
    t = np.linspace(0, 1, c.shape[0])
    return fade(c * (t ** 1.5)[:, None], 0.01, 0.003)


def riser(seconds: float, seed: int = 71, f_lo: float = 350.0, f_hi: float = 9000.0) -> np.ndarray:
    """White-noise riser: band-pass sweeping up exponentially, swelling in level,
    plus a faint rising saw 'siren' for pitch tension."""
    n = int(seconds * SR)
    u = np.linspace(0, 1, n)
    fc = f_lo * (f_hi / f_lo) ** (u ** 1.3)
    out = np.zeros((n, 2))
    for ch in range(2):
        w = noise(n, seed + ch)
        out[:, ch] = tv_biquad(w, "bp", fc * (1.0 + 0.04 * ch), 1.6, block=256)
    out /= np.max(np.abs(out))
    siren = saw(220 * 2 ** (2.0 * u ** 1.6), n) + saw(220.9 * 2 ** (2.0 * u ** 1.6), n)
    siren = tv_biquad(siren, "lp", fc * 0.8, 0.9, block=256) * 0.18
    out += siren[:, None]
    env = u ** 2.2
    return fade(out * env[:, None], 0.05, 0.003)


def untz() -> np.ndarray:
    """The cheeky pitched-down 'untz': a slowed-voice 'uhn' (low glottal buzz
    through /ʌ/ -> /n/ formants) followed by a 'tz' noise burst, with a sub thump."""
    n = int(0.36 * SR)
    t = np.arange(n) / SR
    f0 = 96 * np.exp(-t / 0.35) + 58
    src = 0.7 * saw(f0, n) + 0.3 * pulse(f0, n, 0.2)
    u = np.clip((t - 0.05) / 0.12, 0, 1)
    F1 = 640 - 330 * u
    F2 = 1190 - 190 * u
    F3 = 2390 - 300 * u
    v = (tv_biquad(src, "bp", F1, 4.0, 64) + 0.6 * tv_biquad(src, "bp", F2, 7.0, 64)
         + 0.25 * tv_biquad(src, "bp", F3, 9.0, 64))
    v = tv_biquad(v, "lp", 3000 - 2200 * u, 0.7, 64)  # nasal closure
    venv = np.clip(t / 0.008, 0, 1) * np.where(t < 0.19, 1.0, np.exp(-(t - 0.19) / 0.02))
    tz_env = np.where(t >= 0.2, np.exp(-np.maximum(t - 0.2, 0) / 0.035), 0.0) * np.clip((t - 0.2) / 0.004, 0, 1)
    tz = butter(noise(n, 81), "bp", (5000, 11000), 2) * tz_env
    thump = np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(50 + 60 * np.exp(-t / 0.02)) / SR) * np.exp(-t / 0.08)
    x = v / np.max(np.abs(v)) * venv + 0.5 * tz / np.max(np.abs(tz)) + 0.35 * thump
    return fade(x / np.max(np.abs(x)), 0.001, 0.01)


# ============================================================ tonal voices ==
def supersaw(freq, n: int, voices: int = 7, detune_cents: float = 20.0, width: float = 0.8,
             seed: int = 0) -> np.ndarray:
    """Stereo supersaw: detuned polyBLEP saws with random phases, spread L/R."""
    g = rng(seed)
    offs = np.linspace(-1, 1, voices)
    out = np.zeros((n, 2))
    for o in offs:
        amp = 1.0 if abs(o) < 1e-9 else 0.8
        s = saw(np.asarray(freq) * cents(o * detune_cents), n, g.random()) * amp
        p = o * width
        th = (p + 1) * np.pi / 4
        out[:, 0] += s * np.cos(th)
        out[:, 1] += s * np.sin(th)
    return out / np.sqrt(voices)


def pad_chord(midis, n_gate: int, seed: int, attack=0.04, release=0.35) -> np.ndarray:
    n_tot = n_gate + int(release * SR)
    env = adsr(n_gate, n_tot, a=attack, d=0.5, s=0.85, r=release)
    out = np.zeros((n_tot, 2))
    for i, m in enumerate(midis):
        out += supersaw(midi_to_hz(m), n_tot, voices=5, detune_cents=16, width=0.9, seed=seed * 31 + i)
    return out * env[:, None] / np.sqrt(len(midis))


def lead_saw(midi: float, n_gate: int, seed: int, prev_midi: float | None = None,
             detune=24.0, release=0.09) -> np.ndarray:
    n_tot = n_gate + int(release * SR)
    m = np.full(n_tot, float(midi))
    if prev_midi is not None:
        k = np.exp(-np.arange(n_tot) / (0.012 * SR))
        m = midi + (prev_midi - midi) * k
    env = adsr(n_gate, n_tot, a=0.004, d=0.25, s=0.75, r=release)
    x = supersaw(midi_to_hz(m), n_tot, voices=7, detune_cents=detune, width=0.7, seed=seed)
    return x * env[:, None]


def bass_note(midi_root: int, n_gate: int, accent: float = 1.0) -> np.ndarray:
    """Sub sine one octave under + additive saw layer with a filter-envelope
    (harmonic amplitudes follow a moving 4th-order low-pass response)."""
    rel = int(0.03 * SR)
    n = n_gate + rel
    t = np.arange(n) / SR
    env = adsr(n_gate, n, a=0.003, d=0.12, s=0.8, r=0.03)
    f = midi_to_hz(midi_root)
    sub = np.sin(2 * np.pi * f / 2 * t)
    fc = 260 + 1100 * accent * np.exp(-t / 0.045)
    mid = np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(1, 14):
        fk = 2 * f * 0 + f * k
        if fk > 4000:
            break
        resp = 1.0 / np.sqrt(1.0 + (fk / fc) ** 4)
        mid += np.sin(2 * np.pi * fk * t + 0.3 * k) * resp / k
    return (0.85 * sub + 0.45 * mid) * env


def sub_note(midi_root: int, n_gate: int) -> np.ndarray:
    rel = int(0.25 * SR)
    n = n_gate + rel
    env = adsr(n_gate, n, a=0.05, d=0.4, s=0.9, r=0.25)
    t = np.arange(n) / SR
    f = midi_to_hz(midi_root) / 2
    return (np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * t) + 0.12 * np.sin(4 * np.pi * f * t)) * env


def pluck(midi: float, seconds: float = 0.45, bright: float = 1.0) -> np.ndarray:
    """Additive pluck: saw-like harmonics whose higher partials die faster."""
    n = int(seconds * SR)
    t = np.arange(n) / SR
    f = midi_to_hz(midi)
    x = np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(1, 24):
        fk = f * k
        if fk > 12000:
            break
        tau = 0.32 / (1 + 0.55 * (k - 1) / bright)
        x += np.sin(2 * np.pi * fk * t + 0.7 * k) * np.exp(-t / tau) / k
    x *= 1 - np.exp(-t / 0.0015)
    return fade(x, 0.0, 0.02)


def celesta(midi: float, seconds: float = 2.2) -> np.ndarray:
    """Music-box / celesta: struck-bar partials (slightly stretched) with
    fast-decaying upper modes and a short metallic 'tink'."""
    n = int(seconds * SR)
    t = np.arange(n) / SR
    f = midi_to_hz(midi)
    parts = [(1.0, 1.0, 1.6), (2.0, 0.28, 0.9), (3.01, 0.16, 0.45), (4.18, 0.10, 0.25),
             (5.43, 0.06, 0.14), (8.9, 0.03, 0.05)]
    x = np.zeros(n)
    for r, a, tau in parts:
        if f * r < 16000:
            x += a * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * r * t) * np.exp(-t / tau)
    x += 0.08 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * 11.7 * t) * np.exp(-t / 0.012) if f * 11.7 < 18000 else 0
    x *= 1 - np.exp(-t / 0.0008)
    return fade(x, 0.0, 0.05)


# ======================================================= formant baby cry ==
# Infant-scaled vowel formants (F1..F4, Hz). /a/ and /ɛ/ follow the brief:
# F1 ~700-900, F2 ~1200-1800.
VOWELS = {
    "a": (900, 1450, 3200, 4400),
    "E": (720, 1800, 3300, 4500),   # /ɛ/
    "ae": (950, 1750, 3250, 4500),  # /æ/
    "I": (520, 2150, 3350, 4600),   # /ɪ/
    "i": (380, 2550, 3500, 4700),
    "u": (420, 1050, 3000, 4300),
    "@": (650, 1500, 3100, 4400),   # schwa
}
FORMANT_BW = np.array([150.0, 190.0, 260.0, 330.0])
FORMANT_GAIN = np.array([1.0, 0.7, 0.32, 0.16])
CONSONANT = {  # band centre Hz, length s, level
    "t": (4500, 0.018, 0.5), "k": (2600, 0.025, 0.5), "p": (1400, 0.015, 0.4),
    "th": (6500, 0.04, 0.25), "ts": (7000, 0.05, 0.35), "n": (0, 0, 0),
}


def formant_cry(notes: list[dict], t0: float, seed: int = 0) -> tuple[np.ndarray, int]:
    """Render a monophonic phrase as a formant-synthesised baby voice.

    notes: dicts with onset_s, duration_s, midi, vel, vowels (start, end) and
    optional onset/coda consonants and 'cry' flag (adds the wail pitch arc).
    Returns (mono signal, start sample) for the span starting at t0 - 0.05 s.
    """
    pre = 0.05
    start = int(round((t0 - pre) * SR))
    last = max(nt["onset_s"] + nt["duration_s"] for nt in notes)
    n = int(round((last + 0.25) * SR)) - start
    t = (np.arange(n) + start) / SR
    on = np.array([nt["onset_s"] for nt in notes])
    du = np.array([nt["duration_s"] for nt in notes])
    mi = np.array([nt["midi"] for nt in notes], dtype=float)
    idx = np.clip(np.searchsorted(on, t, side="right") - 1, 0, len(notes) - 1)
    tin = t - on[idx]
    frac = np.clip(tin / du[idx], 0, 1.5)
    # ---- pitch: portamento + scoop + vibrato + cry wail arc
    target = mi[idx]
    pitch = one_pole_lp(target, 0.014)
    pitch[: int(pre * SR)] = mi[0]
    pitch = one_pole_lp(np.concatenate([[mi[0]], target[1:]]), 0.014)
    scoop = -0.8 * np.exp(-tin / 0.035)
    is_cry = np.array([1.0 if nt.get("cry") else 0.0 for nt in notes])[idx]
    wail = is_cry * (0.35 * np.sin(np.pi * np.clip(frac, 0, 1)) - 2.2 * np.clip((frac - 0.45) / 0.55, 0, 1) ** 2)
    vdepth = 0.45 * np.clip((tin - 0.06) / 0.12, 0, 1)
    vib = vdepth * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 6.2 * t)
    jitter = one_pole_lp(noise(n, seed + 1), 0.02) * 0.6
    f0 = midi_to_hz(pitch + scoop + wail + vib + jitter)
    # ---- source: bright pressed phonation (saw + narrow pulse) + breath
    src = 0.65 * saw(f0, n) + 0.35 * pulse(f0, n, 0.28)
    src += 0.10 * butter(noise(n, seed + 2), "hp", 1500, 2)
    # ---- amplitude: per-syllable envelopes (legato with small consonant dips)
    env = np.zeros(n)
    for j, nt in enumerate(notes):
        s = int(round(nt["onset_s"] * SR)) - start
        g = max(int(round(nt["duration_s"] * SR)) - int(0.012 * SR), int(0.03 * SR))
        e = adsr(g, g + int(0.06 * SR), a=0.012, d=0.12, s=0.85, r=0.05) * nt["vel"]
        seg = env[s: s + e.shape[0]]
        np.maximum(seg, e[: seg.shape[0]], out=seg)
    # ---- formant trajectories: vowel start -> end across each syllable
    va = np.array([VOWELS[nt["vowels"][0]] for nt in notes], dtype=float)[idx]
    vb = np.array([VOWELS[nt["vowels"][1]] for nt in notes], dtype=float)[idx]
    mv = np.clip((frac - 0.25) / 0.6, 0, 1)[:, None]
    F = one_pole_lp(va + (vb - va) * mv, 0.018)
    # ---- filter bank: 4 time-varying resonant band-passes in parallel
    x = src * env
    y = np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(4):
        Fk = F[:, k]
        y += FORMANT_GAIN[k] * tv_biquad(x, "bp", Fk, Fk / FORMANT_BW[k], block=64)
    # ---- consonant noise bursts (t/k/p/th onsets, 'ts' coda)
    for j, nt in enumerate(notes):
        for kind, where in ((nt.get("onset"), "on"), (nt.get("coda"), "coda")):
            if not kind or CONSONANT[kind][1] == 0:
                continue
            fc, ln, lvl = CONSONANT[kind]
            m = int(ln * SR)
            burst = biquad(noise(m, seed + 100 + j), "bp", fc, 1.2) * np.exp(-np.arange(m) / (ln * SR / 3))
            s = int(round(nt["onset_s"] * SR)) - start
            if where == "coda":
                s += int(round(nt["duration_s"] * SR)) - m
            if 0 <= s < n:
                seg = y[s: s + m]
                seg += lvl * nt["vel"] * 0.25 * burst[: seg.shape[0]]
    # ---- per-note level normalisation (sparse harmonics vs formant peaks)
    g = np.ones(len(notes))
    rms = []
    for j, nt in enumerate(notes):
        s = int(round((nt["onset_s"] + 0.25 * nt["duration_s"]) * SR)) - start
        e = int(round((nt["onset_s"] + 0.85 * nt["duration_s"]) * SR)) - start
        rms.append(np.sqrt(np.mean(y[s:e] ** 2)) / max(nt["vel"], 1e-3) if e > s else 0)
    rms = np.array(rms)
    ref = np.median(rms[rms > 0])
    g = np.clip(ref / np.maximum(rms, 1e-9), 0.5, 2.0)
    gain = one_pole_lp(g[idx], 0.012)
    y *= gain
    return fade(y, 0.005, 0.05), start

Now the declarative spec (song.py) and the timeline/event expansion it relies on.

BashWrite timeline and spec expansion module
input
description Write timeline and spec expansion module
cat > /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/synth/timeline.py <<'EOF'
"""Timeline: the beat grid, section layout, and expansion of the declarative
song spec into timed events. The renderer and song.json both read the events
produced here, so audio and metadata share one source of truth.

Times are exact multiples of a 16th (15/128 s at 128 BPM, exactly
representable in float64); sample positions are always round(t * sr).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

from dsp import note_to_midi, midi_to_hz

VEL = {"X": 1.0, "x": 0.8, "o": 0.45, ".": 0.0, "-": 0.0}


# --------------------------------------------------------------- helpers ----
def per_bar(n: int, default: str | None = None, **overrides: str | None) -> list[str | None]:
    """A list of n per-bar patterns. Overrides use 1-based bar keys such as
    b5='...' or b5_8='...' (bars 5 through 8 inclusive)."""
    out = [default] * n
    for k, v in overrides.items():
        rng = k[1:].split("_")
        a = int(rng[0])
        b = int(rng[1]) if len(rng) > 1 else a
        for i in range(a, b + 1):
            out[i - 1] = v
    return out


def chords_every(names: list[str], bars_each: int, total: int) -> list[str]:
    seq = [c for c in names for _ in range(bars_each)]
    return [seq[i % len(seq)] for i in range(total)]


CHORD_QUALITY = {"": (0, 4, 7), "m": (0, 3, 7), "sus4": (0, 5, 7), "sus2": (0, 2, 7)}


def chord_pcs(name: str) -> tuple[int, tuple[int, ...]]:
    root = name[0]
    rest = name[1:]
    acc = 0
    if rest[:1] in ("#", "b"):
        acc = 1 if rest[0] == "#" else -1
        rest = rest[1:]
    pc = (note_to_midi(root + "4") - 60 + acc) % 12
    return pc, tuple((pc + iv) % 12 for iv in CHORD_QUALITY[rest])


def chord_notes(name: str, lo: int, hi: int) -> list[int]:
    """All chord tones with lo <= midi < hi."""
    _, pcs = chord_pcs(name)
    return [m for m in range(lo, hi) if m % 12 in pcs]


def bass_root(name: str, lo: int = 40) -> int:
    pc, _ = chord_pcs(name)
    return lo + ((pc - lo) % 12)


# ------------------------------------------------------------ data model ----
@dataclass
class Section:
    name: str
    bars: int
    chords: list[str]
    label: str = ""
    drums: dict[str, list[str | None]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    drum_gain: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)   # inst -> gain or (start, end) ramp
    bass: list[str | None] | None = None          # step patterns, root of current chord
    sub: bool = False                              # sustained sub under each chord
    pad: dict | None = None                        # gain, cutoff points, rhythm (stabs)
    arp: dict | None = None                        # voice, pattern(s), gain, register
    melody: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    fx: list[tuple] = field(default_factory=list)
    cut_last_beats: float = 0.0                    # tonal layers stop this many beats early

    def __post_init__(self):
        assert len(self.chords) == self.bars, (self.name, len(self.chords), self.bars)
        for k, v in self.drums.items():
            assert len(v) == self.bars, (self.name, k, len(v))


@dataclass
class Score:
    bpm: float
    sr: int
    sections: list[dict]
    total_bars: int
    music_end_s: float
    drum_hits: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]]
    chords: list[dict]
    bass: list[dict]
    subs: list[dict]
    pad: list[dict]
    pad_cutoff: list[tuple[float, float]]
    pad_gain: list[tuple[float, float]]
    arps: list[dict]
    melody: list[dict]
    fx: list[dict]


class Grid:
    def __init__(self, bpm: float, sr: int, beats_per_bar: int = 4):
        self.bpm, self.sr, self.bpb = bpm, sr, beats_per_bar
        self.beat = 60.0 / bpm
        self.bar = self.beat * beats_per_bar
        self.step = self.beat / 4  # 16th

    def t(self, bar: float, step16: float = 0.0) -> float:
        return bar * self.bar + step16 * self.step

    def sample(self, t: float) -> int:
        return int(round(t * self.sr))


# ------------------------------------------------------------- expansion ----
def _ramp(v, frac: float) -> float:
    if isinstance(v, tuple):
        return v[0] + (v[1] - v[0]) * frac
    return float(v)


def expand(sections: list[Section], bpm: float, sr: int, syllables: dict) -> Score:
    g = Grid(bpm, sr)
    sec_rows, chords, bass, subs, pad, arps, melody, fx = [], [], [], [], [], [], [], []
    drum_hits: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]] = {}
    cutoff_pts: list[tuple[float, float]] = []
    gain_pts: list[tuple[float, float]] = []
    bar0 = 0
    for si, s in enumerate(sections):
        t0, t1 = g.t(bar0), g.t(bar0 + s.bars)
        tone_end = t1 - s.cut_last_beats * g.beat
        sec_rows.append(dict(name=s.name, label=s.label or s.name, start_bar=bar0, bars=s.bars,
                             start_s=t0, end_s=t1, start_sample=g.sample(t0), end_sample=g.sample(t1),
                             chords=list(s.chords)))
        # ---- drums: a pattern of N chars divides its bar into N equal steps
        for inst, pats in s.drums.items():
            for bi, p in enumerate(pats):
                if not p:
                    continue
                nst = len(p)
                for k, ch in enumerate(p):
                    v = VEL.get(ch, 0.0)
                    if v <= 0:
                        continue
                    t = g.t(bar0 + bi) + k * g.bar / nst
                    frac = (t - t0) / (t1 - t0)
                    v *= _ramp(s.drum_gain.get(inst, 1.0), frac)
                    drum_hits.setdefault(inst, []).append((t, v))
        # ---- chords (merge repeats into held segments)
        bi = 0
        while bi < s.bars:
            bj = bi
            while bj + 1 < s.bars and s.chords[bj + 1] == s.chords[bi]:
                bj += 1
            ct0, ct1 = g.t(bar0 + bi), min(g.t(bar0 + bj + 1), tone_end)
            chords.append(dict(chord=s.chords[bi], start_s=ct0, end_s=g.t(bar0 + bj + 1), section=s.name))
            if s.sub:
                subs.append(dict(t=ct0, dur=ct1 - ct0, midi=bass_root(s.chords[bi]), section=s.name))
            if s.pad and not s.pad.get("rhythm"):
                pad.append(dict(t=ct0, dur=ct1 - ct0, notes=chord_notes(s.chords[bi], *s.pad.get("range", (55, 72))),
                                vel=1.0, section=s.name))
            bi = bj + 1
        # ---- pad stabs
        if s.pad and s.pad.get("rhythm"):
            gate = s.pad.get("gate_steps", 2)
            for b, p in enumerate(per_bar_list(s.pad["rhythm"], s.bars)):
                if not p:
                    continue
                for k, ch in enumerate(p):
                    v = VEL.get(ch, 0)
                    t = g.t(bar0 + b) + k * g.bar / len(p)
                    if v > 0 and t < tone_end:
                        pad.append(dict(t=t, dur=min(gate * g.step, tone_end - t),
                                        notes=chord_notes(s.chords[b], *s.pad.get("range", (55, 72))),
                                        vel=v, section=s.name))
        if s.pad:
            for bar, hz in s.pad.get("cutoff", [(0, 8000.0)]):
                cutoff_pts.append((g.t(bar0 + bar), float(hz)))
            gi = s.pad.get("gain", 1.0)
            gi = gi if isinstance(gi, tuple) else (gi, gi)
            gain_pts += [(t0, gi[0]), (t1 - 1e-6, gi[1])]
        else:
            gain_pts += [(t0, 0.0), (t1 - 1e-6, 0.0)]
        # ---- bass: step patterns playing the current chord root
        if s.bass:
            for b, p in enumerate(s.bass):
                if not p:
                    continue
                nst = len(p)
                for k, ch in enumerate(p):
                    v = VEL.get(ch, 0)
                    t = g.t(bar0 + b) + k * g.bar / nst
                    if v > 0 and t < tone_end:
                        bass.append(dict(t=t, dur=min(0.85 * g.bar / nst * (2 if nst <= 8 else 1), tone_end - t),
                                         midi=bass_root(s.chords[b]), vel=v, section=s.name))
        # ---- arpeggios: digits index the chord tones inside `range`
        if s.arp:
            pats = per_bar_list(s.arp["pattern"], s.bars)
            gain = s.arp.get("gain", 1.0)
            for b, p in enumerate(pats):
                if not p:
                    continue
                tones = chord_notes(s.chords[b], *s.arp.get("range", (64, 81)))
                for k, ch in enumerate(p):
                    if not ch.isdigit():
                        continue
                    t = g.t(bar0 + b) + k * g.bar / len(p)
                    if t >= tone_end:
                        continue
                    frac = (t - t0) / (t1 - t0)
                    arps.append(dict(t=t, midi=tones[int(ch) % len(tones)], vel=_ramp(gain, frac),
                                     voice=s.arp.get("voice", "pluck"), section=s.name))
        # ---- melody placements: lines of (step16, dur16, note, syllable)
        for m in s.melody:
            st = m.get("stretch", 1)
            for li, (line, at_bar) in enumerate(zip(m["lines"], m["bars"])):
                for (step, dur, note, syl) in line["notes"]:
                    t = g.t(bar0 + at_bar, step * st)
                    key = syl.lower()
                    info = syllables.get(key, {})
                    midi = note_to_midi(note) + m.get("transpose", 0)
                    melody.append(dict(
                        onset_s=t, duration_s=dur * st * g.step, midi=midi,
                        freq_hz=float(midi_to_hz(midi)), note=note, syllable=syl,
                        vel=(1.0 if syl.isupper() else 0.82) * m.get("gain", 1.0),
                        line=line["line"], lyric=line["lyric"], voice=m["voice"], section=s.name,
                        vowels=info.get("v", ("a", "E")), onset=info.get("onset"),
                        coda=info.get("coda"), cry=bool(info.get("cry")) and m["voice"] == "cry",
                    ))
        # ---- fx: (kind, bar_offset, length_in_bars[, gain])
        for f in s.fx:
            kind, at, ln = f[0], f[1], f[2]
            gain = f[3] if len(f) > 3 else 1.0
            fx.append(dict(kind=kind, t=g.t(bar0 + at), dur=ln * g.bar, gain=gain, section=s.name))
        bar0 += s.bars
    melody.sort(key=lambda e: (e["voice"], e["onset_s"]))
    for k in drum_hits:
        drum_hits[k].sort()
    return Score(bpm=bpm, sr=sr, sections=sec_rows, total_bars=bar0, music_end_s=g.t(bar0),
                 drum_hits=drum_hits, chords=chords, bass=bass, subs=subs, pad=pad,
                 pad_cutoff=cutoff_pts, pad_gain=gain_pts, arps=arps, melody=melody, fx=fx)


def per_bar_list(p, bars: int) -> list[str | None]:
    if isinstance(p, str) or p is None:
        return [p] * bars
    assert len(p) == bars
    return list(p)

"""It's 2am and I'm Crying -- declarative song spec + CLI entry point.

    uv run --project songs/2am-crying/synth python songs/2am-crying/synth/song.py

renders out/2am_crying.wav (stereo, 44.1 kHz, 24-bit), out/song.json and the
10 s previews out/preview_chorus.wav and out/preview_bridge.wav.

Everything musical lives in this file: tempo, key, the hook melody, drum step
strings, chords per bar and the arrangement. The engine is in timeline.py
(spec -> timed events), instruments.py, fx.py, master.py and render.py.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from timeline import Section, chords_every, per_bar

TITLE = "It's 2am and I'm Crying"
BPM = 128
SR = 44100
KEY = "A minor (final chorus lifts to C major)"
TARGET_LUFS = -10.0
CEILING_DBTP = -1.2   # limiter ceiling; leaves margin so measured TP <= -1 dBTP

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- drums -----
# One string = one bar. N characters split the bar into N equal steps
# (16 chars = 16ths, 32 = 32nds). X accent, x normal, o ghost, . rest.
KICK = "x...x...x...x..."
CLAP = "....x.......x..."
OHAT = "..x...x...x...x."          # offbeat open hats
CHAT = ".o.o.o.o.o.o.o.o"          # closed hats on the e and a
CH8 = "o.o.o.o.o.o.o.o."           # bridge: soft straight 8ths
HALF = "........x......."          # half-time backbeat (beat 3)
UNTZ3 = "x...x...x......."         # "untz untz untz" fill on beats 1-3
ROLL4 = "x...x...x...x..."
ROLL8 = "x.x.x.x.x.x.x.x."
ROLL16 = "xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx"
ROLL32_CUT = "X" * 24 + "." * 8    # 32nds for three beats, then silence
FILL = "............xxxx"

# bass: offbeat 8ths in verses, rolling 16ths (skip the kick) in the drop
BASS_OFF = "..x...x...x...x."
BASS_ROLL = ".xXx.xXx.xXx.xXx"
PAD_STAB = "x.....x.....x..."          # 1, 2&, 4: sparse verse stabs
ARP = "0.1.2.1.3.1.2.1."
ARP16 = "0121312101213121"
LULLABY = "0...1...2...1..."

# ------------------------------------------------------------ the hook -----
# (step16 relative to the line's downbeat, duration in 16ths, note, syllable)
# Scansion: "it's" is a pickup; TWO/THREE on beat 1, a-M on 1& and 2,
# and-I'm on 2& and 3, CRY on 4, -ing on 4&. Collision rule: line 1's pickup
# sits on the and-of-4 (8th) because the bar before it is free; lines 2 and 3
# follow a line whose "-ing" owns the and-of-4, so "-ing" shrinks to a 16th
# and "it's" moves to the "a" of 4. Line 3 has two extra syllables and
# stumbles into 16ths at the top of its bar. A minor pentatonic throughout
# (A C D E G), so the same melody also works over the C-major lift.
LINE1 = dict(line=1, lyric="It's 2am and I'm crying", notes=[
    (-2, 2, "E4", "it's"),
    (0, 2, "A4", "TWO"), (2, 2, "A4", "a"), (4, 2, "C5", "M"), (6, 2, "A4", "and"),
    (8, 4, "C5", "I'm"), (12, 2, "E5", "CRY"), (14, 1, "D5", "ing"),
])
LINE2 = dict(line=2, lyric="It's 3am and I'm crying", notes=[
    (-1, 1, "C5", "it's"),
    (0, 2, "C5", "THREE"), (2, 2, "C5", "a"), (4, 2, "D5", "M"), (6, 2, "C5", "and"),
    (8, 4, "D5", "I'm"), (12, 2, "A5", "CRY"), (14, 1, "G5", "ing"),
])
LINE3 = dict(line=3, lyric="It's 11am and I'm napping", notes=[
    (-1, 1, "E5", "it's"),
    (0, 1, "D5", "e"), (1, 1, "E5", "LEV"), (2, 1, "D5", "en"), (3, 1, "C5", "a"),
    (4, 2, "A4", "M"), (6, 2, "G4", "and"), (8, 4, "A4", "I'm"),
    (12, 2, "G4", "NAP"), (14, 2, "E4", "ping"),
])
# bar 4 of every hook phrase is a rest for the lead (the "untz" fill answers)
HOOK = dict(lines=[LINE1, LINE2, LINE3], bars=[0, 1, 2])

# Vowel (start, end) and consonants for the formant voice, keyed by syllable.
SYLLABLES = {
    "it's": dict(v=("I", "I"), coda="ts"),
    "two": dict(v=("u", "u"), onset="t"),
    "three": dict(v=("i", "i"), onset="th"),
    "a": dict(v=("E", "i")),
    "m": dict(v=("E", "@")),
    "and": dict(v=("ae", "@")),
    "i'm": dict(v=("a", "I")),
    "cry": dict(v=("a", "E"), onset="k", cry=True),   # the "waaah"
    "ing": dict(v=("I", "I")),
    "e": dict(v=("I", "I")),
    "lev": dict(v=("E", "E")),
    "en": dict(v=("@", "@")),
    "nap": dict(v=("ae", "ae")),
    "ping": dict(v=("I", "I"), onset="p"),
}


def hook_at(bars, voices=("cry", "saw"), **kw):
    """Place the 3-line hook phrase at each bar offset in `bars` for each voice."""
    out = []
    for b in bars:
        for v in voices:
            out.append(dict(HOOK, bars=[b + x for x in HOOK["bars"]], voice=v, **kw))
    return out


# --------------------------------------------------------- arrangement -----
VERSE_CHORDS = ["Am", "F", "C", "G"]
DROP_CHORDS = ["Am", "F", "C", "G"]
LIFT_CHORDS = ["C", "G", "Am", "F"]          # final chorus: hopeful C-major axis
BUILD_CHORDS = ["F", "F", "G", "G", "Am", "Am", "E", "E"]


def build(name: str) -> Section:
    return Section(
        name, 8, BUILD_CHORDS, label="pre-chorus / build",
        drums=dict(
            kick=per_bar(8, KICK, b5_8=None),
            clap=per_bar(8, CLAP, b5_8=None),
            ohat=per_bar(8, OHAT, b7_8=None),
            snare=per_bar(8, ROLL4, b3_4=ROLL8, b5_7=ROLL16, b8=ROLL32_CUT),
        ),
        drum_gain=dict(snare=(0.25, 1.0), ohat=0.8),
        bass=dict(pattern=per_bar(8, BASS_OFF, b5_8=None)),
        sub=False,
        pad=dict(gain=(0.55, 0.9), cutoff=[(0, 500), (8, 7000)]),
        arp=dict(voice="pluck", pattern=per_bar(8, ARP, b5_7=ARP16, b8=ARP16[:12] + "...."), gain=(0.6, 1.0)),
        fx=[("riser", 0, 8 - 0.25), ("revcym", 7, 1)],
        cut_last_beats=1.0,
    )


def chorus(name: str, chords: list[str], final: bool = False) -> Section:
    voices = ("cry", "saw", "saw_hi") if final else ("cry", "saw")
    return Section(
        name, 16, chords_every(chords, 1, 16), label="final chorus (C-major lift)" if final else "chorus / drop",
        drums=dict(
            kick=per_bar(16, KICK),
            clap=per_bar(16, CLAP),
            ohat=per_bar(16, OHAT),
            chat=per_bar(16, CHAT),
            untz=per_bar(16, None, b4=UNTZ3, b8=UNTZ3, b12=UNTZ3, b16=UNTZ3),
            snare=per_bar(16, None, b16=FILL),
        ),
        drum_gain=dict(untz=0.9),
        bass=dict(pattern=BASS_ROLL, gate=0.9),
        pad=dict(gain=1.0, cutoff=[(0, 9000)]),
        melody=hook_at([0, 4, 8, 12], voices),
        fx=[("crash", 0, 1), ("crash", 8, 1, 0.6)] + ([("crash", 4, 1, 0.5), ("crash", 12, 1, 0.5)] if final else []),
    )


SECTIONS = [
    Section("intro", 4, chords_every(VERSE_CHORDS, 1, 4), label="intro",
            drums=dict(ohat=per_bar(4, OHAT), chat=per_bar(4, None, b3_4=CHAT)),
            drum_gain=dict(ohat=(0.45, 0.8)),
            pad=dict(gain=(0.5, 0.8), cutoff=[(0, 280), (4, 1600)]),
            fx=[("revcym", 3, 1, 0.8)]),
    Section("verse1", 16, chords_every(VERSE_CHORDS, 2, 16), label="verse 1",
            drums=dict(kick=per_bar(16, KICK), ohat=per_bar(16, OHAT),
                       chat=per_bar(16, None, b9_16=CHAT), clap=per_bar(16, None, b9_16=CLAP),
                       untz=per_bar(16, None, b8=UNTZ3, b16=UNTZ3)),
            bass=dict(pattern=BASS_OFF),
            pad=dict(gain=0.55, cutoff=[(0, 1200)], rhythm=PAD_STAB, gate_steps=3),
            arp=dict(voice="pluck", pattern=per_bar(16, None, b9_16=ARP), gain=0.7)),
    build("build1"),
    chorus("chorus1", DROP_CHORDS),
    Section("verse2", 8, chords_every(VERSE_CHORDS, 2, 8), label="verse 2",
            drums=dict(kick=per_bar(8, KICK), clap=per_bar(8, CLAP), ohat=per_bar(8, OHAT),
                       chat=per_bar(8, CHAT), untz=per_bar(8, None, b4=UNTZ3, b8=UNTZ3)),
            bass=dict(pattern=BASS_OFF),
            pad=dict(gain=0.6, cutoff=[(0, 1500)], rhythm=PAD_STAB, gate_steps=3),
            arp=dict(voice="pluck", pattern=ARP, gain=0.8)),
    build("build2"),
    chorus("chorus2", DROP_CHORDS),
    Section("bridge", 8, chords_every(["C", "Am", "F", "G"], 2, 8), label="bridge: 11am and I'm napping",
            drums=dict(clap=per_bar(8, HALF, b7_8=None), chat=per_bar(8, CH8, b7_8=None),
                       snare=per_bar(8, None, b7=ROLL8, b8=ROLL32_CUT)),
            drum_gain=dict(clap=0.45, chat=0.5, snare=(0.0, 0.9)),
            sub=True,
            pad=dict(gain=0.6, cutoff=[(0, 650), (6, 650), (8, 6000)]),
            arp=dict(voice="celesta", pattern=per_bar(8, None, b5_6=LULLABY, b7=LULLABY), gain=0.55,
                     range=(57, 76)),
            melody=[dict(lines=[LINE3, LINE3], bars=[0, 2], stretch=2, voice="celesta", transpose=12),
                    dict(lines=[LINE3], bars=[2], stretch=2, voice="celesta", transpose=0, gain=0.5)],
            fx=[("riser", 6, 2 - 0.25, 0.9), ("revcym", 7, 1)],
            cut_last_beats=1.0),
    chorus("final", LIFT_CHORDS, final=True),
    Section("outro", 4, ["C", "G", "Am", "Am"], label="outro",
            drums=dict(untz=per_bar(4, None, b4="x...............")),
            drum_gain=dict(untz=0.8),
            pad=dict(gain=(0.8, 0.0), cutoff=[(0, 5000), (4, 600)]),
            melody=[dict(lines=[LINE1], bars=[0], stretch=2, voice="celesta", transpose=12, gain=0.8)],
            fx=[("crash", 0, 1, 0.7)]),
]


def main(argv=None):
    import argparse
    from pathlib import Path

    from render import render_song

    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "out"))
    ap.add_argument("--no-previews", action="store_true")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    render_song(Path(a.out), previews=not a.no_previews)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

BashWrite renderer and dry-run spec expansion11:33 PM

"""Renderer: Score events -> instrument buses -> sidechain/reverb/delay ->
master -> WAV + song.json + previews."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import soundfile as sf

import instruments as ins
from dsp import SR, add_at, biquad, butter, fade, to_stereo, tv_biquad
from fx import apply_gr, convolve_reverb, make_ir, pingpong, sidechain_gain_reduction
from master import master_chain
from timeline import Grid, Score, expand

TAIL_S = 3.0

# Bus levels (linear) and routing. Tuned across the iterations in ITERATIONS.md.
MIX = dict(
    kick=1.0, clap=0.42, ohat=0.20, chat=0.11, snare=0.32, untz=0.50,
    crash=0.22, revcym=0.30, riser=0.26,
    bass=0.50, sub=0.45, pad=0.26, pluck=0.22, celesta=0.34,
    cry=0.55, saw=0.16, saw_hi=0.10,
    reverb=0.32, delay=0.20,
)
SIDECHAIN = dict(bass=0.85, sub=0.6, pad=0.9, pluck=0.5, lead=0.25, reverb=0.6, delay=0.5, fx=0.3)
REVERB_SEND = dict(clap=0.18, snare=0.25, pad=0.12, pluck=0.25, celesta=0.5, lead=0.22, untz=0.05, ohat=0.04)
DELAY_SEND = dict(lead=0.16, celesta=0.25)
PAN = dict(ohat=0.18, chat=-0.22, snare=0.0, untz=0.0)


def _log_interp(t: np.ndarray, pts: list[tuple[float, float]]) -> np.ndarray:
    ts = np.array([p[0] for p in pts])
    vs = np.log(np.array([p[1] for p in pts]))
    o = np.argsort(ts, kind="stable")
    return np.exp(np.interp(t, ts[o], vs[o]))


def _lin_interp(t, pts):
    ts = np.array([p[0] for p in pts])
    vs = np.array([p[1] for p in pts])
    o = np.argsort(ts, kind="stable")
    return np.interp(t, ts[o], vs[o])


def _phrases(events: list[dict], gap: float = 0.3) -> list[list[dict]]:
    out, cur = [], []
    for e in events:
        if cur and e["onset_s"] - (cur[-1]["onset_s"] + cur[-1]["duration_s"]) > gap:
            out.append(cur)
            cur = []
        cur.append(e)
    if cur:
        out.append(cur)
    return out


def render_buses(score: Score, timer) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
    g = Grid(score.bpm, score.sr)
    n = g.sample(score.music_end_s + TAIL_S)
    B = {k: np.zeros((n, 2)) for k in
         ["kick", "drums", "bass", "pad", "pluck", "celesta", "lead", "fx"]}
    sends = {k: np.zeros((n, 2)) for k in ["reverb", "delay"]}

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- drums ----
    shots = dict(kick=ins.kick(), clap=ins.clap(), ohat=ins.hat(0.075, 31), chat=ins.hat(0.022, 33),
                 snare=ins.snare(), untz=ins.untz())
    for inst, hits in score.drum_hits.items():
        shot = to_stereo(shots[inst], PAN.get(inst, 0.0))
        bus = B["kick"] if inst == "kick" else B["drums"]
        for t, v in hits:
            add_at(bus, g.sample(t), shot, MIX[inst] * v)
            if inst in REVERB_SEND:
                add_at(sends["reverb"], g.sample(t), shot, MIX[inst] * v * REVERB_SEND[inst])
    timer("drums")

    # ----------------------------------------------------------- bass ----
    for e in score.bass:
        x = ins.bass_note(e["midi"], g.sample(e["dur"]), accent=e["vel"])
        add_at(B["bass"], g.sample(e["t"]), to_stereo(x), MIX["bass"] * e["vel"])
    sub_bus = np.zeros((n, 2))
    for e in score.subs:
        add_at(sub_bus, g.sample(e["t"]), to_stereo(ins.sub_note(e["midi"], g.sample(e["dur"]))), MIX["sub"])
    timer("bass")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ pad ----
    for i, e in enumerate(score.pad):
        stab = e["dur"] < g.beat
        x = ins.pad_chord(e["notes"], g.sample(e["dur"]), seed=i, attack=0.004 if stab else 0.04,
                          release=0.18 if stab else 0.35)
        add_at(B["pad"], g.sample(e["t"]), x, MIX["pad"] * e["vel"])
    t_axis = np.arange(n) / SR
    cutoff = _log_interp(t_axis, score.pad_cutoff)
    B["pad"] = butter(B["pad"], "hp", 140.0, 2)
    B["pad"] = tv_biquad(B["pad"], "lp", cutoff, 1.1, block=256)
    B["pad"] *= _lin_interp(t_axis, score.pad_gain)[:, None]
    sends["reverb"] += B["pad"] * REVERB_SEND["pad"]
    timer("pad")

    # ----------------------------------------------------------- arps ----
    pluck_cache, cel_cache = {}, {}
    for i, e in enumerate(score.arps):
        if e["voice"] == "pluck":
            x = pluck_cache.setdefault(e["midi"], ins.pluck(e["midi"]))
            add_at(B["pluck"], g.sample(e["t"]), to_stereo(x, 0.3 if i % 2 else -0.3), MIX["pluck"] * e["vel"])
        else:
            x = cel_cache.setdefault(e["midi"], ins.celesta(e["midi"]))
            add_at(B["celesta"], g.sample(e["t"]), to_stereo(x, 0.25 if i % 2 else -0.25), MIX["celesta"] * e["vel"])
    sends["reverb"] += B["pluck"] * REVERB_SEND["pluck"]
    timer("arps")

    # --------------------------------------------------------- melody ----
    by_voice: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
    for e in score.melody:
        by_voice.setdefault(e["voice"], []).append(e)
    for ph_i, ph in enumerate(_phrases(by_voice.get("cry", []))):
        y, s0 = ins.formant_cry(ph, ph[0]["onset_s"], seed=ph_i)
        y = y / max(np.percentile(np.abs(y), 99.7), 1e-9) * 0.8
        add_at(B["lead"], s0, to_stereo(y), MIX["cry"])
    timer("cry lead")
    for voice, shift in (("saw", 0), ("saw_hi", 12)):
        prev_end, prev_m = -1.0, None
        for i, e in enumerate(by_voice.get(voice, [])):
            glide = prev_m if abs(e["onset_s"] - prev_end) < 0.02 else None
            x = ins.lead_saw(e["midi"] + shift, g.sample(e["duration_s"] * 0.92), seed=i,
                             prev_midi=None if glide is None else glide + shift)
            add_at(B["lead"], g.sample(e["onset_s"]), x, MIX[voice] * e["vel"])
            prev_end, prev_m = e["onset_s"] + e["duration_s"], e["midi"]
    for i, e in enumerate(by_voice.get("celesta", [])):
        x = cel_cache.setdefault(e["midi"], ins.celesta(e["midi"]))
        add_at(B["celesta"], g.sample(e["onset_s"]), to_stereo(x, 0.1 if i % 2 else -0.1), MIX["celesta"] * e["vel"])
    B["lead"] = butter(B["lead"], "hp", 180.0, 2)
    sends["reverb"] += B["lead"] * REVERB_SEND["lead"] + B["celesta"] * REVERB_SEND["celesta"]
    sends["delay"] += B["lead"] * DELAY_SEND["lead"] + B["celesta"] * DELAY_SEND["celesta"]
    timer("leads")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------- fx ----
    crash = ins.crash()
    for e in score.fx:
        if e["kind"] == "riser":
            x, gain = ins.riser(e["dur"], seed=int(e["t"])), MIX["riser"]
        elif e["kind"] == "revcym":
            x, gain = ins.reverse_cymbal(e["dur"], seed=int(e["t"])), MIX["revcym"]
        elif e["kind"] == "crash":
            x, gain = crash, MIX["crash"]
        else:
            raise ValueError(e["kind"])
        add_at(B["fx"], g.sample(e["t"]), x, gain * e["gain"])
    timer("fx")

    # ------------------------------------- sidechain, reverb, delay ------
    gr = sidechain_gain_reduction(B["kick"].mean(axis=1) * np.sqrt(2), threshold_db=-14.0, ratio=4.0,
                                  attack_ms=1.5, release_ms=160.0)
    for k in ("bass", "pad", "pluck", "lead", "fx"):
        B[k] = apply_gr(B[k], gr, SIDECHAIN[k])
    sub_bus = apply_gr(sub_bus, gr, SIDECHAIN["sub"])
    B["bass"] += sub_bus
    timer("sidechain")
    ir = make_ir()
    rev = convolve_reverb(butter(sends["reverb"], "hp", 220.0, 2), ir) * MIX["reverb"]
    B["reverb"] = apply_gr(rev, gr, SIDECHAIN["reverb"])
    dl = pingpong(sends["delay"], 0.75 * g.beat, feedback=0.42, repeats=6) * MIX["delay"]
    B["delay"] = apply_gr(dl, gr, SIDECHAIN["delay"])
    timer("reverb+delay")
    B["gr_db"] = gr
    return B


def build_song_json(score: Score, n_samples: int, previews: dict) -> dict:
    g = Grid(score.bpm, score.sr)
    nbeats = score.total_bars * 4
    beats = [i * g.beat for i in range(nbeats)]
    melody = []
    for e in sorted(score.melody, key=lambda e: e["onset_s"]):
        melody.append(dict(onset_s=e["onset_s"], duration_s=e["duration_s"], onset_sample=g.sample(e["onset_s"]),
                           note=e["note"], midi=e["midi"], freq_hz=round(e["freq_hz"], 3), syllable=e["syllable"],
                           line=e["line"], lyric=e["lyric"], voice=e["voice"], section=e["section"],
                           primary=e["primary"]))
    hook = [m for m in melody if m["primary"]]
    lines = []
    for m in hook:
        if not lines or m["line"] != lines[-1]["line"] or m["onset_s"] - lines[-1]["_last"] > 0.5:
            lines.append(dict(line=m["line"], lyric=m["lyric"], section=m["section"], voice=m["voice"],
                              start_s=m["onset_s"], _last=m["onset_s"]))
        lines[-1]["_last"] = m["onset_s"]
        lines[-1]["end_s"] = m["onset_s"] + m["duration_s"]
    for l in lines:
        del l["_last"]
    return dict(
        title="It's 2am and I'm Crying", bpm=score.bpm, sample_rate=score.sr, beats_per_bar=4,
        beat_s=g.beat, bar_s=g.bar, key="A minor", total_bars=score.total_bars,
        music_end_s=score.music_end_s, duration_s=n_samples / score.sr, duration_samples=n_samples,
        time_origin="t=0 is the first sample of the WAV and the downbeat of bar 1; onset_sample = round(t*sr)",
        sections=score.sections,
        chords=score.chords,
        beats=beats, downbeats=beats[::4],
        kicks=[t for t, _ in score.drum_hits.get("kick", [])],
        claps=[t for t, _ in score.drum_hits.get("clap", [])],
        untz=[t for t, _ in score.drum_hits.get("untz", [])],
        hook_lines=lines,
        hook_notes=hook,
        melody_events=melody,
        previews=previews,
    )


def render_song(out_dir: Path, previews: bool = True, name: str = "2am_crying") -> dict:
    import song

    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    T0 = time.time()
    last = [T0]

    def timer(label):
        now = time.time()
        print(f"  {label:<14} {now - last[0]:6.2f} s")
        last[0] = now

    score = expand(song.SECTIONS, song.BPM, song.SR, song.SYLLABLES)
    timer("expand")
    B = render_buses(score, timer)
    gr = B.pop("gr_db")
    mix = sum(B.values())
    print(f"  pre-master peak {20*np.log10(np.max(np.abs(mix))):.1f} dBFS")
    y, minfo = master_chain(mix, song.TARGET_LUFS, song.CEILING_DBTP)
    timer("master")
    g = Grid(score.bpm, score.sr)
    # fade the very end of the reverb tail
    y[-int(0.5 * SR):] *= np.linspace(1, 0, int(0.5 * SR))[:, None]
    wav = out_dir / f"{name}.wav"
    sf.write(wav, y, SR, subtype="PCM_24")
    prev = {}
    if previews:
        secs = {s["name"]: s for s in score.sections}
        for key, sec in (("chorus", "chorus1"), ("bridge", "bridge")):
            st = secs[sec]["start_s"] - g.beat
            a, b = g.sample(st), g.sample(st + 10.0)
            clip = fade(y[a:b].copy(), 0.02, 0.4)
            p = out_dir / f"preview_{key}.wav"
            sf.write(p, clip, SR, subtype="PCM_24")
            prev[key] = dict(file=p.name, start_s=st, end_s=st + 10.0, section=sec)
    meta = build_song_json(score, y.shape[0], prev)
    meta["master"] = minfo
    meta["render_seconds"] = round(time.time() - T0, 2)
    (out_dir / "song.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta, indent=1))
    timer("write")
    print(f"rendered {wav} ({y.shape[0]/SR:.2f} s) in {time.time() - T0:.1f} s")
    return meta
