"""Analyse a rendered WAV: levels, loudness, band balance, clipping, sync
against song.json, and spectrogram PNGs.

    uv run --project songs/2am-crying/synth python songs/2am-crying/synth/analyze.py \
        songs/2am-crying/synth/out/2am_crying.wav [--json .../song.json] [--tag iter3]
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import soundfile as sf
from scipy import signal

from loudness import (integrated_lufs, k_weighted_power, sample_peak_dbfs, true_peak_dbtp,
                      ungated_lufs)

BANDS = [("sub", 0, 60), ("bass", 60, 250), ("low-mid", 250, 2000), ("high-mid", 2000, 6000),
         ("air", 6000, None)]


def band_levels(x: np.ndarray, sr: int) -> dict[str, float]:
    """RMS level per band in dBFS (0 dB = full-scale square wave); bands sum to total."""
    f, p = signal.welch(x, sr, nperseg=8192, axis=0, scaling="density")
    p = p.mean(axis=1) if p.ndim == 2 else p
    df = f[1] - f[0]
    out = {}
    for name, lo, hi in BANDS:
        m = (f >= lo) & (f < (hi if hi else sr / 2 + 1))
        out[name] = 10 * np.log10(max(np.sum(p[m]) * df, 1e-20))
    return out


def clipping(x: np.ndarray, thresh: float = 0.9999) -> tuple[int, int]:
    hot = np.abs(x) >= thresh
    count = int(hot.sum())
    runs = 0
    for ch in range(x.shape[1]):
        h = hot[:, ch].astype(np.int8)
        runs += int(np.sum(np.diff(np.concatenate([[0], h, [0]])) == 1))
    return count, runs


def sync_check(mono: np.ndarray, sr: int, times: list[float], win_ms: float = 20.0):
    """Locate each scheduled kick by cross-correlating against the kick one-shot."""
    try:
        from instruments import kick
        tpl = kick()[: int(0.06 * sr)]
    except Exception:  # analyser used outside this project
        return None
    tpl = tpl - tpl.mean()
    W = int(win_ms / 1000 * sr)
    offs, scores = [], []
    for t in times:
        s = int(round(t * sr))
        a, b = s - W, s + W + len(tpl)
        if a < 0 or b > len(mono):
            continue
        seg = mono[a:b]
        c = np.correlate(seg, tpl, mode="valid")
        energy = np.sqrt(np.convolve(seg ** 2, np.ones(len(tpl)), mode="valid") * np.sum(tpl ** 2))
        nc = c / np.maximum(energy, 1e-12)
        k = int(np.argmax(nc))
        offs.append((k - W) / sr * 1000)
        scores.append(nc[k])
    offs, scores = np.array(offs), np.array(scores)
    good = scores > 0.5
    return dict(n=len(offs), matched=int(good.sum()),
                median_ms=float(np.median(np.abs(offs[good]))) if good.any() else None,
                max_ms=float(np.max(np.abs(offs[good]))) if good.any() else None,
                mean_corr=float(scores.mean()) if len(scores) else None)


def spectrogram_png(x: np.ndarray, sr: int, path: Path, meta: dict | None, t0: float = 0.0,
                    t1: float | None = None, nfft: int = 4096, hop: int = 1024, title: str = "",
                    beats: bool = False):
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    mono = x.mean(axis=1)
    a = int(t0 * sr)
    b = int((t1 if t1 is not None else len(mono) / sr) * sr)
    seg = mono[a:b]
    f, tt, Z = signal.stft(seg, sr, nperseg=nfft, noverlap=nfft - hop, boundary=None, padded=False)
    S = 20 * np.log10(np.abs(Z) * 2 + 1e-9)
    tt = tt + t0
    keep = (f >= 25) & (f <= 20000)
    fig_h = 7.5 if not beats else 6.5
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 1, figsize=(16, fig_h), gridspec_kw=dict(height_ratios=[4, 1]), sharex=True)
    ax = axes[0]
    ax.pcolormesh(tt, f[keep], S[keep], shading="auto", cmap="magma", vmin=-95, vmax=-15, rasterized=True)
    ax.set_yscale("log")
    ax.set_ylim(25, 20000)
    ax.set_yticks([30, 60, 120, 250, 500, 1000, 2000, 4000, 8000, 16000])
    ax.set_yticklabels(["30", "60", "120", "250", "500", "1k", "2k", "4k", "8k", "16k"])
    ax.set_ylabel("Hz")
    ax.set_title(title)
    # short-term loudness (3 s window, 0.1 s hop) underneath
    pw = k_weighted_power(x[a:b], sr)
    w, h = int(3.0 * sr) if not beats else int(0.4 * sr), int(0.1 * sr) if not beats else int(0.01 * sr)
    c = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(pw)])
    st = np.arange(0, max(len(pw) - w, 1), h)
    lt = -0.691 + 10 * np.log10(np.maximum((c[st + w] - c[st]) / w, 1e-12))
    axes[1].plot(t0 + (st + w / 2) / sr, lt, color="#c0392b", lw=1.2)
    axes[1].set_ylabel("LUFS (3 s)" if not beats else "LUFS (400 ms)")
    axes[1].set_ylim(-40, 0)
    axes[1].grid(alpha=0.3)
    axes[1].set_xlabel("seconds")
    if meta:
        for s in meta["sections"]:
            if t0 <= s["start_s"] <= tt[-1]:
                for axx in axes:
                    axx.axvline(s["start_s"], color="cyan", lw=1.0, alpha=0.8)
                ax.text(s["start_s"] + 0.3, 17000, s["name"], color="cyan", fontsize=9, va="top")
        if beats:
            for bt in meta["beats"]:
                if t0 <= bt <= tt[-1]:
                    ax.axvline(bt, color="white", lw=0.5, alpha=0.35)
            for m in meta["hook_notes"]:
                if t0 <= m["onset_s"] <= tt[-1]:
                    ax.text(m["onset_s"], 28, m["syllable"], color="white", fontsize=8, rotation=90, va="bottom")
    ax.set_xlim(tt[0], tt[-1])
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(path, dpi=100)
    plt.close(fig)


def main(argv=None):
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("wav")
    ap.add_argument("--json", default=None, help="song.json (default: next to the WAV)")
    ap.add_argument("--png", default=None, help="spectrogram path (default: <wav dir>/spectrogram.png)")
    ap.add_argument("--zoom-section", default="chorus1")
    ap.add_argument("--tag", default=None, help="also save copies as <dir>/iterations/<tag>_*.png")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    wav = Path(a.wav)
    info = sf.info(str(wav))
    x, sr = sf.read(str(wav), always_2d=True)
    jpath = Path(a.json) if a.json else wav.with_name("song.json")
    meta = json.loads(jpath.read_text()) if jpath.exists() else None

    dur = len(x) / sr
    peak = sample_peak_dbfs(x)
    tp = true_peak_dbtp(x)
    lufs = integrated_lufs(x, sr)
    rms = 10 * np.log10(np.mean(x ** 2))
    crest = peak - rms
    clip_n, clip_runs = clipping(x)
    bands = band_levels(x, sr)

    print(f"file:              {wav}")
    print(f"format:            {info.samplerate} Hz, {info.channels} ch, {info.subtype}")
    print(f"duration:          {int(dur // 60)}:{dur % 60:05.2f}  ({dur:.3f} s)")
    print(f"sample peak:       {peak:6.2f} dBFS")
    print(f"true peak (4x):    {tp:6.2f} dBTP")
    print(f"integrated:        {lufs:6.2f} LUFS  (approx. BS.1770 gated)")
    print(f"RMS:               {rms:6.2f} dBFS")
    print(f"crest factor:      {crest:6.2f} dB  (sample peak - RMS)")
    print(f"PLR:               {tp - lufs:6.2f} dB  (true peak - integrated)")
    print(f"clipping:          {clip_n} samples at |x| >= 0.9999 ({clip_runs} runs)")
    print("band energy (RMS dBFS, 0 dB = full-scale square):")
    for k, v in bands.items():
        print(f"  {k:<9} {v:7.2f}")
    if meta:
        pw = k_weighted_power(x, sr)
        print("per-section loudness (ungated K-weighted) and peak:")
        for s in meta["sections"]:
            a0, b0 = int(s["start_s"] * sr), int(s["end_s"] * sr)
            print(f"  {s['name']:<8} {s['start_s']:7.2f}-{s['end_s']:7.2f} s  {s['bars']:3d} bars  "
                  f"{ungated_lufs(pw[a0:b0]):6.2f} LUFS  peak {sample_peak_dbfs(x[a0:b0]):6.2f} dBFS")
        sc = sync_check(x.mean(axis=1), sr, meta.get("kicks", []))
        if sc:
            print(f"sync check:        {sc['matched']}/{sc['n']} scheduled kicks found in the master; "
                  f"|offset| median {sc['median_ms']:.3f} ms, max {sc['max_ms']:.3f} ms "
                  f"(mean corr {sc['mean_corr']:.2f})")
        if abs(meta["duration_s"] - dur) > 1e-6:
            print(f"WARNING: song.json duration {meta['duration_s']} != wav {dur}")
    png = Path(a.png) if a.png else wav.with_name("spectrogram.png")
    spectrogram_png(x, sr, png, meta, title=f"{wav.name}  |  {lufs:.1f} LUFS, TP {tp:.1f} dBTP")
    pngs = [png]
    if meta:
        sec = {s["name"]: s for s in meta["sections"]}.get(a.zoom_section)
        if sec:
            z = png.with_name(png.stem + "_" + a.zoom_section + ".png")
            t0 = sec["start_s"] - meta["beat_s"]
            spectrogram_png(x, sr, z, meta, t0=t0, t1=t0 + 4 * meta["bar_s"] + meta["beat_s"], nfft=2048,
                            hop=128, title=f"{a.zoom_section}: first 4 bars (beat grid, hook syllables)",
                            beats=True)
            pngs.append(z)
    if a.tag:
        d = png.parent / "iterations"
        d.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        import shutil
        for p in pngs:
            shutil.copy(p, d / f"{a.tag}_{p.name}")
    print("spectrograms:      " + ", ".join(str(p) for p in pngs))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

cat > /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/synth/ITERATIONS.md <<'EOF'
# Iteration log

Each entry is one full loop: `song.py` render -> `analyze.py` -> read the
spectrogram PNGs -> change the spec/synth. Snapshots of every iteration's
spectrograms are in `out/iterations/<tag>_*.png`. Verdicts about sound are
read off spectrograms and numbers (no listening was possible in this loop);
a human should audition `out/preview_chorus.wav` and `out/preview_bridge.wav`.

Band levels are RMS dBFS per band (0 dB = full-scale square): sub <60,
bass 60-250, low-mid 250-2k, high-mid 2k-6k, air >6k.

## iter0: baseline (first full render)

What: first complete pass of all instruments, sidechain, convolution reverb,
ping-pong delay, soft clip + true-peak limiter with auto-gain.

Numbers: 198.0 s file (195.0 s of music + 3 s tail), render 10.5 s,
-10.03 LUFS, TP -1.20 dBTP, crest 10.0 dB, 0 clipped samples.
Bands: sub -16.7, bass -13.8, low-mid -19.1, high-mid -33.0, air -33.0.
Sections: intro -23.0, verse1 -10.5, build -11.7, chorus -9.4, verse2 -10.2,
bridge -9.9 (!), final -9.3, outro -15.7 LUFS (ungated).
Sync: 320/320 kicks found, median offset 0.20 ms, max 0.25 ms.

Spectrogram: everything below ~120 Hz is saturated for the whole track; the
bass/sub drive the limiter, so mids and highs are pushed down (high-mid and
air ~19 dB under the bass band). Verses are as dense as the drops, so there is
almost no lift into the chorus (1 LU). The bridge has a wall of sub energy
(sustained, un-ducked sub notes) and is as loud as a chorus, which is wrong for a
no-kick lullaby breakdown. Chorus zoom: the formant lead is clearly visible:
the fundamental runs 330-880 Hz with vibrato, scoops and the falling "CRY" wail, and
harmonics 2-3 are boosted where F1/F2 sit. Kick pitch sweeps show as the curved
streaks at every beat. Syllable labels are unreadable (white on yellow).
EOF

BashPer-bus loudness and band diagnostic
input
description Per-bus loudness and band diagnostic
timeout 300000
cat > /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jalex-projects-clawdpop/ca2089be-6e29-4607-84a4-ba095fc3fca1/scratchpad/busdiag.py <<'EOF'
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, '/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/synth')
import numpy as np
import song
from timeline import expand
from render import render_buses
from analyze import band_levels
from loudness import k_weighted_power, ungated_lufs
sc = expand(song.SECTIONS, song.BPM, song.SR, song.SYLLABLES)
B = render_buses(sc, lambda l: None)
B.pop('gr_db')
secs = {s['name']: s for s in sc.sections}
for secname in sys.argv[1:] or ['chorus1']:
    s = secs[secname]
    a, b = int(s['start_s']*44100), int(s['end_s']*44100)
    print(f"== {secname} (pre-master, unnormalised)")
    print(f"{'bus':<8} {'LUFS':>7} {'sub':>7} {'bass':>7} {'lowmid':>7} {'himid':>7} {'air':>7}")
    tot = sum(B.values())[a:b]
    for k, x in list(B.items()) + [('TOTAL', None)]:
        seg = tot if x is None else x[a:b]
        if np.max(np.abs(seg)) < 1e-6:
            continue
        bl = band_levels(seg, 44100)
        L = ungated_lufs(k_weighted_power(seg, 44100))
        print(f"{k:<8} {L:7.1f} " + " ".join(f"{v:7.1f}" for v in bl.values()))
EOF

bridge -14.5, final -8.5, outro -18.1 LUFS. There is now a real arc: the drop
lands about 4 LU above verse 1, and the bridge is a breakdown.
Sync: max 0.09 ms.

Spectrogram: the 3 s loudness strip now traces the arrangement (intro swell,
flat verse, build ramp, drop plateau, bridge dip to ~-18, riser, final drop,
outro fade). Upper bands are visibly denser in the drops. Still visible: the
bridge sub is a bright block from 40 to 65 Hz. Problem found afterwards with a
gain-reduction probe: the sidechain was only pumping **5 dB** at the kick and
1.6 dB on the offbeat, because lowering the kick bus also lowered the
detector's key. A solo render of the lead bus (cry + saw double) showed
clean harmonics, no aliasing, and the CRY wail falls. However, the saw double fills
the formant valleys.
EOF

## iter3: sidechain key normalised, cry gets its own bus + voice break

Changed: sidechain key is the kick bus divided by its mix gain, so pump depth
is independent of kick level; the cry lead moved to its own `cry` bus (so
`song.py --solo cry` works) with a +4 dB presence peak @ 3.5 kHz; saw double

period-doubling subharmonic ("voice break") in the back half of every CRY.
Analyzer: wider colour range (-100..-8 dB) and a 50 ms loudness strip on the
zoom so per-beat pumping is visible.

Numbers: -10.14 LUFS, TP -1.20, crest 11.25 dB, render 10.9 s.

Spectrogram: the 50 ms loudness strip now has a saw-tooth swell every beat, and

## v3: the day (lead version)

Inspiration: the "Imma Be" day-in-my-life selfie trend. The baby is filming its own day on
a phone, front camera, one clip per timestamp, caption in the corner. Every line is
"It's [time] and I'm [doing]". No verses in the old sense: the hook is the whole song,
the verses are the same form at double speed filling in the rest of the day, and the day
is a loop (the last line is the first line). Jokes come from the rhythm: cry, cry, cry,
nap; cry, poop, poop again.

**[Intro]** *(phone camera click; spoken, bright, to the camera)*
> Hi. It's me. Here's my day.
> *(untz. untz. untz. untz.)*

**[Chorus]**
> It's 2am and I'm crying
> It's 3am and I'm crying
> It's 4am and I'm crying
> It's 11am and I'm napping
> It's 12pm and I'm crying
> It's 1pm and I'm pooping
> It's 2pm and I'm pooping again

**[Verse 1: morning, double-time]**
> It's 5am and I'm quiet. It's 5:45 and I'm up.
> It's 6am and I'm standing. It's 6:15 and I'm still standing.
> It's 7am and I'm drinking. It's 7:30 and I'm wearing it.
> It's 8am and I'm eating. It's 8:05 and I'm throwing it.
> It's 9am and I'm reading. It's 9:01 and I'm eating it.
> It's 10am and I'm pointing. It's 10:30 and I'm pointing at the fan.
> It's 10:45 and I'm fighting it.
> It's 11am and I'm napping.

**[Chorus]** *(with stutter ad-libs)*
> It's 2am and I'm crying *(cry-cry-crying)*
> It's 3am and I'm crying *(cry-cry-crying)*
> It's 4am and I'm crying
> It's 11am and I'm napping *(nap-nap-napping)*
> It's 12pm and I'm crying
> It's 1pm and I'm pooping
> It's 2pm and I'm pooping again

**[Verse 2: afternoon to night, double-time]**
> It's 3pm and I'm napping. It's 4pm and I'm drinking.
> It's 5pm and I'm crying. It's 5:30 and I'm crying at the fan.
> It's 6pm and I'm eating. It's 6:05 and the floor is eating.
> It's 7pm and I'm splashing. It's 7:15 and I'm naked and I'm running.
> It's 7:30 and I'm reading. It's 7:45 and I'm "sleeping."
> It's 8pm and I'm sleeping. It's 9pm and I'm sleeping.
> It's 10pm and *you're* sleeping. It's 11pm and we're sleeping.
> It's 12am and I'm sleeping. It's 1am and I'm...
> *(one bar of silence)*

**[Bridge: the bottle and the nap]** *(half-time, music box, soft)*
> It's 10:50 and I'm drinking.
> It's 10:55 and I'm drinking, and it's warm, and I'm heavy.
> It's 10:58 and I'm heavy.
> It's 11am and I'm napping.
> *(I'm napping... I'm napping...)*

**[Final Chorus]** *(biggest; the whole day in one pass)*
> It's 2am and I'm crying
> It's 3am and I'm crying
> It's 4am and I'm crying
> It's 11am and I'm napping
> It's 12pm and I'm crying
> It's 1pm and I'm pooping
> It's 2pm and I'm pooping again
> It's 3pm and I'm napping
> It's 7pm and I'm splashing
> It's 8pm and I'm sleeping
> It's 1am and I'm...

**[Outro]** *(spoken, same as the intro; the loop closes)*
> Hi. It's me. Here's my day.
> *(click)*

### Notes

- The user's seven lines are the chorus, verbatim. Everything else is the same form.
- The times follow a real two-nap day: up before 6, nap around 11, nap around 3, bath at 7,
  down by 8. The night section ends in "sleeping" lines that get sleepier, then the
  1am ellipsis, then 2am and the loop.
- "You're sleeping" at 10pm is the one line where the camera turns around.
- The bottle verse from v2 is compressed into the bridge: drinking → warm → heavy → napping.
- For a 60-second TikTok cut: intro, chorus, verse 2 from "8pm", outro.
BashWrite lyric fitter module and test layouts11:51 PM
input
description Write lyric fitter module and test layouts
cat > /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/synth/lyrics.py <<'EOF'
"""Lyric setting: turns syllabified "It's [time] and I'm [doing]" clauses into
timed melody notes on the hook rhythm, plus per-syllable vowels/consonants for
the formant voice.

Line format: groups separated by "|", syllables by spaces or hyphens,
stressed syllables in CAPS:

    "it's | TWO a M | and I'm | CRY-ing"
      pickup  time     mid      verb (+ tail)

Hook rhythm (16th steps from the line's downbeat, before rate scaling):
pickup "it's" on the and-of-4 of the previous bar (-2); the time syllables on
1, 1& and 2 (more syllables stumble into 16ths: e-LEV-en-a-M = 0,1,2,3,4);
"and" on 2&, "I'm" on 3 (held); the verb's stressed syllable on 4. The verb
group fills whatever room is left before the next line's pickup: 8ths if
there is space, otherwise 16ths, then 32nds. Lines in consecutive bars
therefore get "CRY" on 4 and "-ing" on the e of 4, keeping the next "it's" on
the and-of-4. rate=0.5 is double time (two clauses per bar), rate=2 half time.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

TIME_SLOTS = {1: [0], 2: [0, 2], 3: [0, 2, 4], 4: [0, 1, 2, 4], 5: [0, 1, 2, 3, 4], 6: [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5]}
MID_SLOTS = {0: [], 1: [8], 2: [6, 8], 3: [6, 8, 10], 4: [6, 7, 8, 10], 5: [6, 7, 8, 9, 10],
             6: [6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 11]}
PICKUP_STEP = -2.0   # the and-of-4, in absolute 16ths (not rate-scaled)
VERB_STEP = 12


def split_syllables(group: str) -> list[str]:
    out = []
    for word in group.split():
        out += [s for s in word.split("-") if s]
    return out


def parse(line: str) -> dict:
    parts = [p.strip() for p in line.split("|")]
    parts += [""] * (4 - len(parts))
    pickup, time, mid, verb = (split_syllables(p) for p in parts[:4])
    text = " ".join(" ".join(p.split()) for p in parts if p).replace(" | ", " ")
    return dict(pickup=pickup, time=time, mid=mid, verb=verb, text=text)


def _resample(seq: list, n: int) -> list:
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    if n == 1:
        return [seq[0]]
    L = len(seq)
    return [seq[min(L - 1, int(round(i * (L - 1) / (n - 1))))] for i in range(n)]


def layout(line: dict, rate: float, verb_end_steps: float, contour: dict,
           verb_steps: list[float] | None = None) -> list[tuple[float, float, str, str, str]]:
    """Return [(step, dur, note, syllable, group)] in absolute 16ths relative to
    the line's downbeat. verb_end_steps is where the next line's pickup sits."""
    ev = []
    for s in line["pickup"]:
        ev.append([PICKUP_STEP, contour["pickup"], s, "pickup"])
    for st, nt, s in zip(TIME_SLOTS[len(line["time"])] if line["time"] else [],
                         _resample(contour["time"], len(line["time"])), line["time"]):
        ev.append([st * rate, nt, s, "time"])
    for st, nt, s in zip(MID_SLOTS[len(line["mid"])], _resample(contour["mid"], len(line["mid"])), line["mid"]):
        ev.append([st * rate, nt, s, "mid"])
    nv = len(line["verb"])
    if nv:
        start = VERB_STEP * rate
        if verb_steps is not None:
            steps = [x for x in verb_steps]
        else:
            room = verb_end_steps - start
            d = 0.25
            for cand in (2 * rate, 1 * rate, 0.5 * rate, 0.25):
                if (nv - 1) * cand + min(cand, 1.0) <= room + 1e-9:
                    d = cand
                    break
            steps = [start + i * d for i in range(nv)]
        for st, nt, s in zip(steps, _resample(contour["verb"], nv), line["verb"]):
            ev.append([st, nt, s, "verb"])
    ev.sort(key=lambda e: e[0])
    out = []
    for i, (st, nt, s, grp) in enumerate(ev):
        nxt = ev[i + 1][0] if i + 1 < len(ev) else None
        if nxt is not None:
            dur = nxt - st
        else:  # last syllable: ring until the next line (capped at a half note)
            dur = max(min(verb_end_steps - st, 8.0), 0.5)
        out.append((st, dur, nt, s, grp))
    return out


# ------------------------------------------------------------ phonetics ----
# syllable -> (vowel start, vowel end, onset consonant, coda consonant)
PHON = {
    "it's": ("I", "I", None, "ts"), "two": ("u", "u", "t", None), "three": ("i", "i", "th", None),
    "four": ("@", "@", "f", None), "five": ("a", "I", "f", None), "six": ("I", "I", "s", "ts"),
    "sev": ("E", "E", "s", None), "en": ("@", "@", None, None), "eight": ("E", "i", None, "t"),
    "nine": ("a", "I", None, None), "ten": ("E", "E", "t", None), "e": ("I", "I", None, None),
    "lev": ("E", "E", None, None), "twelve": ("E", "E", "t", None), "one": ("@", "@", None, None),
    "a": ("E", "i", None, None), "m": ("E", "@", None, None), "p": ("i", "i", "p", None),
    "for": ("@", "@", "f", None), "ty": ("i", "i", "t", None), "fif": ("I", "I", "f", None),
    "teen": ("i", "i", "t", None), "thir": ("@", "@", "th", None), "oh": ("@", "u", None, None),
    "and": ("ae", "@", None, None), "i'm": ("a", "I", None, None), "you're": ("u", "@", None, None),
    "we're": ("i", "@", None, None), "the": ("@", "@", "th", None), "floor": ("@", "@", "f", None),
    "is": ("I", "I", None, "s"), "cry": ("a", "E", "k", None), "ing": ("I", "I", None, None),
    "nap": ("ae", "ae", None, "p"), "ping": ("I", "I", "p", None), "poo": ("u", "u", "p", None),
    "gain": ("E", "i", "k", None), "qui": ("a", "I", "k", None), "et": ("@", "@", None, "t"),
    "up": ("@", "@", None, "p"), "stand": ("ae", "ae", "s", None), "still": ("I", "I", "s", None),
    "drink": ("I", "I", "t", None), "wear": ("E", "@", None, None), "it": ("I", "I", None, "t"),
    "eat": ("i", "i", None, "t"), "throw": ("@", "u", "th", None), "read": ("i", "i", None, None),
    "point": ("@", "I", "p", None), "at": ("ae", "ae", None, "t"), "fan": ("ae", "ae", "f", None),
    "fight": ("a", "I", "f", "t"), "splash": ("ae", "ae", "s", "sh"), "na": ("E", "i", None, None),
    "ked": ("I", "I", "k", None), "run": ("@", "@", None, None), "sleep": ("i", "i", "s", "p"),
    "warm": ("@", "@", None, None), "heav": ("E", "E", "f", None), "y": ("i", "i", None, None),
    "hi": ("a", "I", "f", None), "me": ("i", "i", None, None), "here's": ("i", "@", "f", "s"),
    "my": ("a", "I", None, None), "day": ("E", "i", "t", None),
}


def phonetics(syl: str) -> dict:
    k = syl.lower().strip(".,!?\"'()…").replace("...", "")
    v0, v1, on, coda = PHON.get(k, ("a", "E", None, None))
    return dict(v=(v0, v1), onset=on, coda=coda, cry=(k == "cry"))

cat > /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/synth/timeline.py <<'EOF'
"""Timeline: the beat grid, section layout, and expansion of the declarative
song spec into timed events. The renderer and song.json both read the events
produced here, so audio and metadata share one source of truth.

Times are exact multiples of a 64th note at 128 BPM (all exactly
representable in float64); sample positions are always round(t * sr).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

from dsp import midi_to_hz, note_to_midi
from lyrics import layout, parse, phonetics, pickup_step

VEL = {"X": 1.0, "x": 0.8, "o": 0.45, ".": 0.0, "-": 0.0}
NOTE_NAMES = ["C", "C#", "D", "D#", "E", "F", "F#", "G", "G#", "A", "A#", "B"]


def midi_name(m: int) -> str:
    return f"{NOTE_NAMES[m % 12]}{m // 12 - 1}"


# --------------------------------------------------------------- helpers ----
def per_bar(n: int, default: str | None = None, **overrides: str | None) -> list[str | None]:
    """A list of n per-bar patterns. Overrides use 1-based bar keys such as
    b5='...' or b5_8='...' (bars 5 through 8 inclusive)."""
    out = [default] * n
    for k, v in overrides.items():
        rng = k[1:].split("_")
        a = int(rng[0])
        b = int(rng[1]) if len(rng) > 1 else a
        for i in range(a, b + 1):
            out[i - 1] = v
    return out


def per_bar_list(p, bars: int) -> list[str | None]:
    if isinstance(p, str) or p is None:
        return [p] * bars
    assert len(p) == bars, (len(p), bars)
    return list(p)


def chords_every(names: list[str], bars_each: int, total: int) -> list[str]:
    seq = [c for c in names for _ in range(bars_each)]
    return [seq[i % len(seq)] for i in range(total)]


CHORD_QUALITY = {"": (0, 4, 7), "m": (0, 3, 7), "sus4": (0, 5, 7), "sus2": (0, 2, 7)}


def chord_pcs(name: str) -> tuple[int, tuple[int, ...]]:
    root, rest, acc = name[0], name[1:], 0
    if rest[:1] in ("#", "b"):
        acc = 1 if rest[0] == "#" else -1
        rest = rest[1:]
    pc = (note_to_midi(root + "4") - 60 + acc) % 12
    return pc, tuple((pc + iv) % 12 for iv in CHORD_QUALITY[rest])


def chord_notes(name: str, lo: int, hi: int) -> list[int]:
    _, pcs = chord_pcs(name)
    return [m for m in range(lo, hi) if m % 12 in pcs]


def bass_root(name: str, lo: int = 40) -> int:
    pc, _ = chord_pcs(name)
    return lo + ((pc - lo) % 12)


def chord_tone_below(name: str, midi: int, gap: int = 3) -> int:
    _, pcs = chord_pcs(name)
    m = midi - gap
    while m % 12 not in pcs:
        m -= 1
    return m


# ------------------------------------------------------------ data model ----
@dataclass
class Section:
    name: str
    bars: int
    chords: list[str]
    label: str = ""
    kind: str = ""
    drums: dict[str, list[str | None]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    drum_gain: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)   # inst -> gain or (start, end) ramp
    bass: dict | None = None          # pattern(s) + gate (16ths), plays the chord root
    sub: bool = False                 # sustained sub under each chord
    pad: dict | None = None           # gain, cutoff points, rhythm (stabs)
    brass: dict | None = None         # electro-hop synth-brass stabs: pattern(s), gain
    arp: dict | None = None           # voice, pattern(s), gain, register
    lyrics: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    fx: list[tuple] = field(default_factory=list)
    music_lp: list[tuple[float, float]] | None = None   # (bar, Hz) low-pass on the band
    cut_last_beats: float = 0.0       # tonal layers stop this many beats early

    def __post_init__(self):
        assert len(self.chords) == self.bars, (self.name, len(self.chords), self.bars)
        for k, v in self.drums.items():
            assert len(v) == self.bars, (self.name, k, len(v))


@dataclass
class Score:
    bpm: float
    sr: int
    sections: list[dict]
    total_bars: int
    music_end_s: float
    drum_hits: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]]
    chords: list[dict]
    bass: list[dict]
    subs: list[dict]
    pad: list[dict]
    pad_cutoff: list[tuple[float, float]]
    pad_gain: list[tuple[float, float]]
    brass: list[dict]
    arps: list[dict]
    melody: list[dict]
    lyric_lines: list[dict]
    adlibs: list[dict]
    fx: list[dict]
    music_lp: list[tuple[float, float]]


class Grid:
    def __init__(self, bpm: float, sr: int, beats_per_bar: int = 4):
        self.bpm, self.sr, self.bpb = bpm, sr, beats_per_bar
        self.beat = 60.0 / bpm
        self.bar = self.beat * beats_per_bar
        self.step = self.beat / 4  # 16th

    def t(self, bar: float, step16: float = 0.0) -> float:
        return bar * self.bar + step16 * self.step

    def sample(self, t: float) -> int:
        return int(round(t * self.sr))


def _ramp(v, frac: float) -> float:
    if isinstance(v, tuple):
        return v[0] + (v[1] - v[0]) * frac
    return float(v)


def _steps(sec_bars: int, bar0: int, pats, g: Grid, tone_end: float):
    """Yield (bar_index, t, vel) for every hit in per-bar step patterns."""
    for b, p in enumerate(per_bar_list(pats, sec_bars)):
        if not p:
            continue
        for k, ch in enumerate(p):
            v = VEL.get(ch, 0.0)
            t = g.t(bar0 + b) + k * g.bar / len(p)
            if v > 0 and t < tone_end:
                yield b, t, v


# ------------------------------------------------------------- expansion ----
def expand(sections: list[Section], bpm: float, sr: int, contours: dict) -> Score:
    g = Grid(bpm, sr)
    sec_rows, chords, bass, subs, pad, brass, arps, fx = [], [], [], [], [], [], [], []
    drum_hits: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]] = {}
    cutoff_pts: list[tuple[float, float]] = []
    gain_pts: list[tuple[float, float]] = []
    lp_pts: list[tuple[float, float]] = []
    placements = []
    bar_chord: list[str] = []
    bar0 = 0
    for s in sections:
        t0, t1 = g.t(bar0), g.t(bar0 + s.bars)
        tone_end = t1 - s.cut_last_beats * g.beat
        bar_chord += list(s.chords)
        sec_rows.append(dict(name=s.name, label=s.label or s.name, kind=s.kind or s.name,
                             start_bar=bar0, bars=s.bars, start_s=t0, end_s=t1,
                             start_sample=g.sample(t0), end_sample=g.sample(t1), chords=list(s.chords)))
        # ---- drums: a pattern of N chars divides its bar into N equal steps
        for inst, pats in s.drums.items():
            for _, t, v in _steps(s.bars, bar0, pats, g, t1):
                v *= _ramp(s.drum_gain.get(inst, 1.0), (t - t0) / (t1 - t0))
                drum_hits.setdefault(inst, []).append((t, v))
        # ---- chords (merge repeats into held segments)
        bi = 0
        while bi < s.bars:
            bj = bi
            while bj + 1 < s.bars and s.chords[bj + 1] == s.chords[bi]:
                bj += 1
            ct0, ct1 = g.t(bar0 + bi), min(g.t(bar0 + bj + 1), tone_end)
            chords.append(dict(chord=s.chords[bi], start_s=ct0, end_s=g.t(bar0 + bj + 1), section=s.name))
            if ct1 > ct0:
                if s.sub:
                    subs.append(dict(t=ct0, dur=ct1 - ct0, midi=bass_root(s.chords[bi]), section=s.name))
                if s.pad and not s.pad.get("rhythm"):
                    pad.append(dict(t=ct0, dur=ct1 - ct0, notes=chord_notes(s.chords[bi], *s.pad.get("range", (55, 72))),
                                    vel=1.0, section=s.name))
            bi = bj + 1
        # ---- pad stabs / brass stabs / bass
        if s.pad and s.pad.get("rhythm"):
            for b, t, v in _steps(s.bars, bar0, s.pad["rhythm"], g, tone_end):
                pad.append(dict(t=t, dur=min(s.pad.get("gate_steps", 2) * g.step, tone_end - t),
                                notes=chord_notes(s.chords[b], *s.pad.get("range", (55, 72))), vel=v, section=s.name))
        if s.brass:
            for b, t, v in _steps(s.bars, bar0, s.brass["pattern"], g, tone_end):
                notes = chord_notes(s.chords[b], *s.brass.get("range", (55, 74)))
                notes = [bass_root(s.chords[b], 45)] + notes
                brass.append(dict(t=t, dur=min(s.brass.get("gate_steps", 1.5) * g.step, tone_end - t),
                                  notes=notes, vel=v * _ramp(s.brass.get("gain", 1.0), (t - t0) / (t1 - t0)),
                                  section=s.name))
        if s.pad:
            pts = s.pad.get("cutoff", [(0, 8000.0)])
            for bar, hz in pts:
                cutoff_pts.append((g.t(bar0 + bar) - (1e-6 if bar == s.bars else 0.0), float(hz)))
            if pts[-1][0] < s.bars:
                cutoff_pts.append((t1 - 1e-6, float(pts[-1][1])))
            gi = s.pad.get("gain", 1.0)
            gi = gi if isinstance(gi, tuple) else (gi, gi)
            gain_pts += [(t0, gi[0]), (t1 - 1e-6, gi[1])]
        else:
            gain_pts += [(t0, 0.0), (t1 - 1e-6, 0.0)]
        if s.music_lp:
            for bar, hz in s.music_lp:
                lp_pts.append((g.t(bar0 + bar) - (1e-6 if bar == s.bars else 0.0), float(hz)))
        else:
            lp_pts += [(t0, 20000.0), (t1 - 1e-6, 20000.0)]
        if s.bass:
            for b, t, v in _steps(s.bars, bar0, s.bass["pattern"], g, tone_end):
                bass.append(dict(t=t, dur=min(s.bass.get("gate", 1.8) * g.step, tone_end - t),
                                 midi=bass_root(s.chords[b]), vel=v, section=s.name))
        # ---- arpeggios: digits index the chord tones inside `range`
        if s.arp:
            for b, p in enumerate(per_bar_list(s.arp["pattern"], s.bars)):
                if not p:
                    continue
                tones = chord_notes(s.chords[b], *s.arp.get("range", (64, 81)))
                for k, ch in enumerate(p):
                    t = g.t(bar0 + b) + k * g.bar / len(p)
                    if ch.isdigit() and t < tone_end:
                        arps.append(dict(t=t, midi=tones[int(ch) % len(tones)],
                                         vel=_ramp(s.arp.get("gain", 1.0), (t - t0) / (t1 - t0)),
                                         voice=s.arp.get("voice", "pluck"), section=s.name))
        for lp in s.lyrics:
            placements.append(dict(lp, section=s.name, abs_step=(bar0 + lp["bar"]) * 16 + lp.get("step", 0.0)))
        for f in s.fx:
            kind, at, ln = f[0], f[1], f[2]
            fx.append(dict(kind=kind, t=g.t(bar0 + at), dur=ln * g.bar, gain=f[3] if len(f) > 3 else 1.0,
                           section=s.name))
        bar0 += s.bars

    melody, lyric_lines, adlibs = _set_lyrics(placements, contours, bar_chord, g)
    for k in drum_hits:
        drum_hits[k].sort()
    return Score(bpm=bpm, sr=sr, sections=sec_rows, total_bars=bar0, music_end_s=g.t(bar0),
                 drum_hits=drum_hits, chords=chords, bass=bass, subs=subs, pad=pad,
                 pad_cutoff=cutoff_pts, pad_gain=gain_pts, brass=brass, arps=arps, melody=melody,
                 lyric_lines=lyric_lines, adlibs=adlibs, fx=fx, music_lp=lp_pts)


def _first_step(p) -> float:
    if p.get("notes"):
        return p["abs_step"] + min(n[0] for n in p["notes"])
    return p["abs_step"] + pickup_step(p.get("rate", 1.0))


def _set_lyrics(placements, contours, bar_chord, g: Grid):
    """Lay out every sung line on the hook rhythm; each line's verb group gets
    the room left before the next sung line's pickup (across sections)."""
    sung = sorted([p for p in placements if p.get("kind", "sung") != "spoken"], key=lambda p: p["abs_step"])
    melody, lines, adlibs = [], [], []
    for i, p in enumerate(sung):
        rate = p.get("rate", 1.0)
        nxt = sung[i + 1] if i + 1 < len(sung) else None
        verb_end = (_first_step(nxt) - p["abs_step"]) if nxt else 32.0
        verb_end = min(verb_end, p.get("max_end", 32.0))
        if p.get("notes"):
            ev = [(st, du, nt, sy, "custom") for st, du, nt, sy in p["notes"]]
            text = p.get("display", " ".join(e[3] for e in ev))
        else:
            parsed = parse(p["text"])
            ev = layout(parsed, rate, verb_end, contours[p["contour"]], p.get("verb_steps"))
            text = p.get("display", parsed["text"])
        line_id = len(lines)
        line_bar = int(p["abs_step"] // 16)
        voices = p.get("voices", {"cry": 0})
        sylls = []
        for vi, (voice, tr) in enumerate(voices.items()):
            for st, du, nt, sy, grp in ev:
                abs_st = p["abs_step"] + st
                midi = note_to_midi(nt) + tr
                ph = phonetics(sy)
                base = dict(onset_s=abs_st * g.step, duration_s=du * g.step, syllable=sy, group=grp,
                            vel=(1.0 if any(c.isupper() for c in sy) else 0.82) * p.get("gain", 1.0),
                            line_id=line_id, lyric=text, section=p["section"], vowels=ph["v"],
                            onset=ph["onset"], coda=ph["coda"])
                ev_main = dict(base, midi=midi, note=midi_name(midi), voice=voice,
                               cry=ph["cry"] and voice == "cry", primary=(vi == 0))
                melody.append(ev_main)
                if vi == 0:
                    sylls.append(dict(syllable=sy, onset_s=base["onset_s"], duration_s=base["duration_s"],
                                      onset_sample=g.sample(base["onset_s"]), note=midi_name(midi)))
                if voice == "cry" and p.get("harmony"):
                    ch = bar_chord[max(0, min(len(bar_chord) - 1, int(max(abs_st, p["abs_step"]) // 16)))]
                    h1 = chord_tone_below(ch, midi, 3)
                    h2 = chord_tone_below(ch, h1, 3)
                    for hv, hm in (("harm_a", h1), ("harm_b", h2)):
                        melody.append(dict(base, midi=hm, note=midi_name(hm), voice=hv, cry=False, primary=False,
                                           vel=base["vel"] * 0.8))
        lines.append(dict(id=line_id, section=p["section"], text=text, kind=p.get("kind", "sung"),
                          bar=line_bar, downbeat_s=p["abs_step"] // 16 * 16 * g.step + 0.0,
                          rate=rate, start_s=sylls[0]["onset_s"],
                          end_s=sylls[-1]["onset_s"] + sylls[-1]["duration_s"], syllables=sylls,
                          voice=next(iter(voices))))
        if p.get("stutter"):
            # chop the stressed verb syllable 3x then its tail, over the next bar's held "I'm"
            vs = [e for e in ev if e[4] == "verb"]
            src, tail = vs[0], vs[1] if len(vs) > 1 else vs[0]
            nb = (line_bar + 1) * 16
            word = (src[3] + tail[3]).lower()
            adlibs.append(dict(
                text=f"({src[3].lower()}-{src[3].lower()}-{word})", section=p["section"], line_id=line_id,
                src_s=(p["abs_step"] + src[0]) * g.step, src_dur_s=min(src[1], 1.0) * g.step,
                tail_s=(p["abs_step"] + tail[0]) * g.step, tail_dur_s=min(tail[1], 1.0) * g.step,
                hits_s=[(nb + k) * g.step for k in (8, 9, 10)], tail_hit_s=(nb + 11) * g.step))
    for p in placements:
        if p.get("kind") == "spoken":
            st = p["abs_step"] * g.step
            lines.append(dict(id=len(lines), section=p["section"], text=p["text"], kind="spoken",
                              bar=int(p["abs_step"] // 16), downbeat_s=int(p["abs_step"] // 16) * 16 * g.step,
                              rate=None, start_s=st, end_s=st + p.get("beats", 4) * g.beat, syllables=[],
                              voice=None))
    lines.sort(key=lambda l: l["start_s"])
    for i, l in enumerate(lines):
        l["id"] = i
    melody.sort(key=lambda e: (e["voice"], e["onset_s"]))
    return melody, lines, adlibs

def patch(f, pairs):
    s=open(root+f).read()
    for a,b in pairs:
        assert a in s, (f,a)
        s=s.replace(a,b)
    open(root+f,'w').write(s)

'''def camera_click() -> np.ndarray:
    """Phone shutter: two short mechanical transients ~45 ms apart."""
    n = int(0.12 * SR)
    t = np.arange(n) / SR
    x = np.zeros(n)
    for t0, a, f in ((0.0, 1.0, 3200), (0.045, 0.7, 2400)):
        m = t >= t0
        tt = t[m] - t0
        x[m] += a * (biquad(noise(m.sum(), 91 + int(f)), "bp", f, 2.0) * np.exp(-tt / 0.004)
                     + 0.6 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * 0.5 * tt) * np.exp(-tt / 0.006))
    return fade(x / np.max(np.abs(x)), 0.0, 0.01)


# ============================================================ tonal voices =='''),
("""def lead_saw(""",
'''def brass(midis, n_gate: int, seed: int = 0) -> np.ndarray:
    """2010 electro-hop synth brass stab: stacked detuned saws, a pitch 'blip'
    into the note, a snapping filter envelope and saturation."""
    rel = int(0.08 * SR)
    n = n_gate + rel
    t = np.arange(n) / SR
    blip = cents(-60 * np.exp(-t / 0.018))
    out = np.zeros((n, 2))
    for i, m in enumerate(midis):
        out += supersaw(midi_to_hz(m) * blip, n, voices=3, detune_cents=12, width=0.9, seed=seed * 17 + i)
    out /= np.sqrt(len(midis))
    fc = 900 + 5200 * np.exp(-t / 0.07)
    out = tv_biquad(out, "lp", fc, 1.4, block=64)
    env = adsr(n_gate, n, a=0.002, d=0.12, s=0.6, r=0.06)
    y = np.tanh(1.6 * out * env[:, None])
    return y / np.tanh(1.6)


def lead_saw('''),
])

"""It's 2am and I'm Crying -- declarative song spec (v3 "the day") + CLI.

    uv run --project songs/2am-crying/synth python songs/2am-crying/synth/song.py

renders out/2am_crying.wav (stereo, 44.1 kHz, 24-bit, loops seamlessly),
out/song.json and the 10 s previews out/preview_chorus.wav and
out/preview_bridge.wav.

Everything musical lives in this file: tempo, key, lyrics (syllabified),
melodic contours, drum step strings, chords per bar and the arrangement.
The engine is timeline.py + lyrics.py (spec -> timed events), instruments.py,
fx.py, master.py and render.py. Lyrics follow LYRICS.md "v3: the day".
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from timeline import Section, chords_every, per_bar

TITLE = "It's 2am and I'm Crying"
BPM = 128
SR = 44100
KEY = "A minor (final chorus re-harmonised on the C-major axis)"
TARGET_LUFS = -10.0
CEILING_DBTP = -1.2   # limiter ceiling; leaves margin so measured TP <= -1 dBTP
LOOP = True           # wrap the reverb/release tail onto the start: the file loops seamlessly

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- drums -----
# One string = one bar. N characters split the bar into N equal steps
# (16 chars = 16ths, 32 = 32nds). X accent, x normal, o ghost, . rest.
KICK = "x...x...x...x..."
CLAP = "....x.......x..."
OHAT = "..x...x...x...x."          # offbeat open hats
CHAT = ".o.o.o.o.o.o.o.o"          # closed hats on the e and a
CH8 = "o.o.o.o.o.o.o.o."           # bridge: soft straight 8ths
HALF = "........x......."          # half-time backbeat (beat 3)
UNTZ4 = "x...x...x...x..."         # intro/outro: "untz. untz. untz. untz."
UNTZ3 = "x...x...x......."         # fill bar: "untz untz untz"
ROLL8 = "x.x.x.x.x.x.x.x."
ROLL16 = "xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx"
ROLL32_CUT = "X" * 24 + "." * 8    # 32nds for three beats, then silence
FILL = "............xxxx"

BASS_OFF = "..x...x...x...x."          # offbeat 8ths (verses)
BASS_ROLL = ".xXx.xXx.xXx.xXx"          # rolling 16ths that skip the kick (drops)
PAD_STAB = "x.....x.....x..."
BRASS_HIT = "x..............."          # drop: one big stab per bar
BRASS_HOP = "x..x..x...x..x.."          # verses: 2010 electro-hop syncopation
BRASS_FILL = "x..x..x.x.x....."
ARP16 = "0121312101213121"
LULLABY = "0...1...2...1..."

# ------------------------------------------------------ melodic contours ---
# Pitches per lyric group (pickup / time / mid / verb), resampled to the
# number of syllables. A minor pentatonic (A C D E G), so every contour also
# works over the C-major re-harmonisation of the final chorus.
CONTOURS = {
    "cry1": dict(pickup="E4", time=["A4", "A4", "C5"], mid=["A4", "C5"], verb=["E5", "D5"]),
    "cry2": dict(pickup="C5", time=["C5", "C5", "D5"], mid=["C5", "D5"], verb=["A5", "G5"]),
    "cry3": dict(pickup="D5", time=["D5", "D5", "E5"], mid=["D5", "E5"], verb=["C6", "A5"]),
    "nap": dict(pickup="E5", time=["D5", "E5", "D5", "C5", "A4"], mid=["G4", "A4"], verb=["G4", "E4"]),
    "poop": dict(pickup="E4", time=["A4", "A4", "C5"], mid=["A4", "G4"], verb=["D4", "C4"]),
    "poop2": dict(pickup="E4", time=["A4", "A4", "C5"], mid=["A4", "G4"], verb=["D4", "C4", "E4", "G4"]),
    "splash": dict(pickup="C5", time=["C5", "D5", "C5", "D5"], mid=["C5", "D5"], verb=["G5", "E5"]),
    "sleep": dict(pickup="C5", time=["C5", "A4", "A4"], mid=["G4", "A4"], verb=["E4", "D4"]),
    "dots": dict(pickup="E4", time=["A4", "A4", "C5"], mid=["A4", "C5"], verb=["C5"]),
    "v_up": dict(pickup="E4", time=["A4", "C5", "A4"], mid=["C5", "D5"], verb=["E5", "D5", "C5", "A4"]),
    "v_dn": dict(pickup="D5", time=["C5", "A4", "G4"], mid=["A4", "C5"], verb=["A4", "G4", "E4", "D4"]),
    "lull1": dict(pickup="E4", time=["E4", "G4", "A4"], mid=["G4", "E4"], verb=["D4", "C4"]),
    "lull2": dict(pickup="E4", time=["G4", "A4", "C5", "A4"], mid=["G4", "E4"],
                  verb=["D4", "E4", "G4", "A4", "G4", "E4", "D4", "E4", "C4"]),
    "lull3": dict(pickup="D4", time=["E4", "D4", "C4", "A3"], mid=["C4", "D4"], verb=["E4", "D4"]),
}

# --------------------------------------------------------------- lyrics ----
# Groups: pickup | time | mid | verb(+tail). CAPS = stressed syllable.
CHORUS = [
    ("it's | TWO a M | and I'm | CRY-ing", "cry1"),
    ("it's | THREE a M | and I'm | CRY-ing", "cry2"),
    ("it's | FOUR a M | and I'm | CRY-ing", "cry3"),
    ("it's | e-LEV-en a M | and I'm | NAP-ping", "nap"),
    ("it's | TWELVE p M | and I'm | CRY-ing", "cry1"),
    ("it's | ONE p M | and I'm | POO-ping", "poop"),
    ("it's | TWO p M | and I'm | POO-ping a-GAIN", "poop2"),
]
FINAL_EXTRA = [
    ("it's | THREE p M | and I'm | NAP-ping", "nap"),
    ("it's | SEV-en p M | and I'm | SPLASH-ing", "splash"),
    ("it's | EIGHT p M | and I'm | SLEEP-ing", "sleep"),
    ("it's | ONE a M | and I'm... |", "dots"),
]
VERSE1 = [  # morning, double-time: two clauses per bar
    "it's | FIVE a M | and I'm | QUI-et", "it's | FIVE FOR-ty FIVE | and I'm | UP",
    "it's | SIX a M | and I'm | STAND-ing", "it's | SIX fif-TEEN | and I'm | STILL STAND-ing",
    "it's | SEV-en a M | and I'm | DRINK-ing", "it's | SEV-en THIR-ty | and I'm | WEAR-ing it",
    "it's | EIGHT a M | and I'm | EAT-ing", "it's | EIGHT oh FIVE | and I'm | THROW-ing it",
    "it's | NINE a M | and I'm | READ-ing", "it's | NINE oh ONE | and I'm | EAT-ing it",
    "it's | TEN a M | and I'm | POINT-ing", "it's | TEN THIR-ty | and I'm | POINT-ing at the FAN",
    "it's | TEN FOR-ty FIVE | and I'm | FIGHT-ing it",
]
VERSE1_LAST = "it's | e-LEV-en a M | and I'm | NAP-ping"
VERSE2 = [  # afternoon to night, double-time, getting sleepier
    "it's | THREE p M | and I'm | NAP-ping", "it's | FOUR p M | and I'm | DRINK-ing",
    "it's | FIVE p M | and I'm | CRY-ing", "it's | FIVE THIR-ty | and I'm | CRY-ing at the FAN",
    "it's | SIX p M | and I'm | EAT-ing", "it's | SIX oh FIVE | and the FLOOR is | EAT-ing",
    "it's | SEV-en p M | and I'm | SPLASH-ing", "it's | SEV-en fif-TEEN | and I'm NA-ked and I'm | RUN-ning",
    "it's | SEV-en THIR-ty | and I'm | READ-ing", "it's | SEV-en FOR-ty FIVE | and I'm | SLEEP-ing",
    "it's | EIGHT p M | and I'm | SLEEP-ing", "it's | NINE p M | and I'm | SLEEP-ing",
    "it's | TEN p M | and YOU'RE | SLEEP-ing", "it's | e-LEV-en p M | and WE'RE | SLEEP-ing",
]
VERSE2_ACAPPELLA = ["it's | TWELVE a M | and I'm | SLEEP-ing", "it's | ONE a M | and I'm... |"]

CRY = {"cry": 0}
DROP_VOICES = {"cry": 0, "saw": 0}
FINAL_VOICES = {"cry": 0, "saw": 0, "saw_hi": 12}
SPOKEN = "Hi. It's me. Here's my day."


def chorus_lyrics(lines, voices, **kw):
    return [dict(text=t, contour=c, bar=i, voices=voices, **kw) for i, (t, c) in enumerate(lines)]


def double_time(lines, bar0=0, contours=("v_up", "v_dn"), gain=1.0, voices=CRY):
    out = []
    for i, t in enumerate(lines):
        g = gain(i) if callable(gain) else gain
        out.append(dict(text=t, contour=contours[i % 2], bar=bar0 + i // 2, step=8 * (i % 2),
                        rate=0.5, voices=voices, gain=g))
    return out


# --------------------------------------------------------- arrangement -----
DROP = ["Am", "F", "C", "G"]
LIFT = ["C", "G", "Am", "F"]


def intro_like(name: str, label: str) -> Section:
    """Intro and outro are the same material so the file loops seamlessly."""
    return Section(
        name, 2, ["Am", "E"], label=label, kind="intro" if name == "intro" else "outro",
        drums=dict(ohat=per_bar(2, OHAT), untz=per_bar(2, None, b2=UNTZ4)),
        drum_gain=dict(ohat=0.45, untz=0.95),
        pad=dict(gain=0.55, cutoff=[(0, 380), (2, 1500)]),
        lyrics=[dict(text=SPOKEN, kind="spoken", bar=0, beats=4),
                dict(text="(untz. untz. untz. untz.)", kind="spoken", bar=1, beats=4)],
        fx=([("click", 0, 0)] if name == "intro" else []) + [("revcym", 1, 1, 0.8)],
    )


def chorus(name: str, label: str, chords, lyrics, bars: int = 8, stutter=(), final=False) -> Section:
    for i in stutter:
        lyrics[i]["stutter"] = True
    fill = f"b{bars}"
    return Section(
        name, bars, chords_every(chords, 1, bars), label=label, kind="chorus",
        drums=dict(kick=per_bar(bars, KICK), clap=per_bar(bars, CLAP), ohat=per_bar(bars, OHAT),
                   chat=per_bar(bars, CHAT), untz=per_bar(bars, None, **{fill: UNTZ3}),
                   snare=per_bar(bars, None, **{fill: FILL})),
        drum_gain=dict(untz=0.9),
        bass=dict(pattern=BASS_ROLL, gate=0.9),
        pad=dict(gain=1.0, cutoff=[(0, 9000)]),
        brass=dict(pattern=per_bar(bars, BRASS_HIT, **{fill: BRASS_FILL}), gain=1.0 if final else 0.8),
        lyrics=lyrics,
        fx=[("crash", 0, 1), ("crash", 4, 1, 0.6)] + ([("crash", 8, 1, 0.6)] if final else []),
    )


SECTIONS = [
    intro_like("intro", "Intro"),
    chorus("chorus1", "Chorus", DROP, chorus_lyrics(CHORUS, DROP_VOICES)),
    Section("verse1", 8, chords_every(DROP, 1, 8), label="Verse 1: morning, double-time", kind="verse",
            drums=dict(kick=per_bar(8, KICK), ohat=per_bar(8, OHAT), chat=per_bar(8, None, b3_8=CHAT),
                       clap=per_bar(8, CLAP), snare=per_bar(8, None, b8=ROLL16)),
            drum_gain=dict(kick=0.9, snare=0.5),
            bass=dict(pattern=BASS_OFF),
            pad=dict(gain=0.45, cutoff=[(0, 1400)], rhythm=PAD_STAB, gate_steps=3),
            brass=dict(pattern=per_bar(8, BRASS_HOP, b8=None), gain=0.7),
            arp=dict(voice="pluck", pattern=per_bar(8, ARP16, b7_8=None), gain=0.45),
            lyrics=double_time(VERSE1, gain=0.85)
                   + [dict(text=VERSE1_LAST, contour="nap", bar=7, voices=CRY, gain=0.85)],
            fx=[("riser", 6, 2 - 0.25, 0.8), ("revcym", 7, 1)]),
    chorus("chorus2", "Chorus (with stutter ad-libs)", DROP, chorus_lyrics(CHORUS, DROP_VOICES),
           stutter=(0, 1, 3)),
    Section("verse2", 8, chords_every(DROP, 1, 8), label="Verse 2: afternoon to night, double-time", kind="verse",
            drums=dict(kick=per_bar(8, KICK, b8=None), ohat=per_bar(8, OHAT, b7_8=None),
                       chat=per_bar(8, CHAT, b6_8=None), clap=per_bar(8, CLAP, b8=None)),
            drum_gain=dict(kick=(0.95, 0.7), ohat=(0.9, 0.4), chat=(0.8, 0.3), clap=(0.9, 0.5)),
            bass=dict(pattern=BASS_OFF),
            pad=dict(gain=(0.6, 0.4), cutoff=[(0, 2500), (7, 500)], rhythm=PAD_STAB, gate_steps=4),
            brass=dict(pattern=per_bar(8, BRASS_HOP, b6_8=None), gain=(0.6, 0.3)),
            music_lp=[(0, 16000), (7, 450), (8, 450)],
            lyrics=double_time(VERSE2, gain=lambda i: 0.9 - 0.025 * i)
                   + [dict(text=t, contour=c, bar=7, step=8 * k, rate=0.5, voices=CRY, gain=0.55)
                      for k, (t, c) in enumerate(zip(VERSE2_ACAPPELLA, ("sleep", "dots")))],
            cut_last_beats=4.0),   # bar 8: the band is silent; the baby, a cappella
    Section("bridge", 8, chords_every(["F", "C", "Am", "G"], 2, 8), label="Bridge: the bottle and the nap",
            kind="bridge",
            drums=dict(clap=per_bar(8, HALF, b7_8=None), chat=per_bar(8, CH8, b7_8=None),
                       snare=per_bar(8, None, b7=ROLL8, b8=ROLL32_CUT)),
            drum_gain=dict(clap=0.45, chat=0.5, snare=(0.0, 0.9)),
            sub=True,
            pad=dict(gain=0.42, cutoff=[(0, 650), (6, 650), (8, 6000)]),
            arp=dict(voice="celesta", pattern=per_bar(8, LULLABY, b7_8=None), gain=0.4, range=(57, 76)),
            lyrics=[
                dict(text="it's | TEN FIF-ty | and I'm | DRINK-ing", contour="lull1", bar=0, rate=2,
                     voices={"celesta": 12}),
                dict(text="it's | TEN fif-ty FIVE | and I'm | DRINK-ing and it's WARM and I'm HEAV-y",
                     contour="lull2", bar=2, rate=2, voices={"celesta": 12}),
                dict(text="it's | TEN fif-ty EIGHT | and I'm | HEAV-y", contour="lull3", bar=5,
                     voices={"celesta": 12}),
                dict(text="it's | e-LEV-en a M | and I'm | NAP-ping", contour="nap", bar=6,
                     voices={"celesta": 0}),
                dict(display="(I'm napping... I'm napping...)", bar=7, voices={"celesta": 12}, gain=0.6,
                     notes=[(0, 2, "E4", "I'm"), (2, 2, "D4", "NAP"), (4, 2, "C4", "ping"),
                            (6, 2, "E4", "I'm"), (8, 2, "D4", "NAP"), (10, 2, "A3", "ping")]),

            fx=[("riser", 6, 2 - 0.25, 0.9), ("revcym", 7, 1)],

    chorus("final_chorus", "Final Chorus", LIFT,
           chorus_lyrics(CHORUS + FINAL_EXTRA, FINAL_VOICES, harmony=True), bars=12, final=True),
    intro_like("outro", "Outro"),
]


def main(argv=None):
    import argparse
    from pathlib import Path

    from render import render_song

    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "out"))
    ap.add_argument("--no-previews", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--solo", default=None,
                    help="comma list of buses to render alone "
                         "(kick,drums,bass,pad,brass,pluck,celesta,cry,harm,chop,lead,fx,reverb,delay)")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    render_song(Path(a.out), previews=not a.no_previews, solo=a.solo.split(",") if a.solo else None)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""Renderer: Score events -> instrument buses -> sidechain/reverb/delay ->
master -> WAV + song.json + previews."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import soundfile as sf

import instruments as ins
from dsp import SR, add_at, biquad, butter, fade, to_stereo, tv_biquad
from fx import apply_gr, convolve_reverb, make_ir, pingpong, sidechain_gain_reduction
from master import master_chain
from timeline import Grid, Score, expand

TAIL_S = 3.0

# Bus levels (linear) and routing. Tuned across the iterations in ITERATIONS.md.
MIX = dict(
    kick=0.55, clap=0.42, ohat=0.40, chat=0.20, snare=0.30, untz=0.36, click=0.35,
    crash=0.22, revcym=0.30, riser=0.26,
    bass=0.30, sub=0.16, pad=0.30, brass=0.20, pluck=0.22, celesta=0.13,
    cry=0.55, harm=0.30, chop=0.30, saw=0.16, saw_hi=0.12,
    reverb=0.32, delay=0.20,
)
SIDECHAIN = dict(bass=0.85, sub=0.6, pad=0.9, brass=0.6, pluck=0.5, lead=0.25, reverb=0.6, delay=0.5, fx=0.3)
REVERB_SEND = dict(clap=0.18, snare=0.25, pad=0.12, brass=0.10, pluck=0.25, celesta=0.5, lead=0.22, untz=0.05,
                   ohat=0.04, chop=0.3)
DELAY_SEND = dict(lead=0.16, celesta=0.25, chop=0.25)
PAN = dict(ohat=0.18, chat=-0.22, snare=0.0, untz=0.0)
BAND = ("kick", "drums", "bass", "pad", "brass", "pluck", "celesta", "lead", "fx")  # what music_lp filters


def _interp(t: np.ndarray, pts: list[tuple[float, float]], log: bool) -> np.ndarray:
    ts = np.array([p[0] for p in pts])
    vs = np.array([p[1] for p in pts])
    o = np.argsort(ts, kind="stable")
    if log:
        return np.exp(np.interp(t, ts[o], np.log(vs[o])))
    return np.interp(t, ts[o], vs[o])


def _phrases(events: list[dict], gap: float = 0.3) -> list[list[dict]]:
    out, cur = [], []
    for e in events:
        if cur and e["onset_s"] - (cur[-1]["onset_s"] + cur[-1]["duration_s"]) > gap:
            out.append(cur)
            cur = []
        cur.append(e)
    if cur:
        out.append(cur)
    return out


def render_buses(score: Score, timer) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
    g = Grid(score.bpm, score.sr)
    n = g.sample(score.music_end_s + TAIL_S)
    B = {k: np.zeros((n, 2)) for k in
         ["kick", "drums", "bass", "pad", "brass", "pluck", "celesta", "cry", "harm", "chop", "lead", "fx"]}
    sends = {k: np.zeros((n, 2)) for k in ["reverb", "delay"]}
    t_axis = np.arange(n) / SR

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- drums ----
    shots = dict(kick=ins.kick(), clap=ins.clap(), ohat=ins.hat(0.075, 31), chat=ins.hat(0.022, 33),
                 snare=ins.snare(), untz=ins.untz())
    for inst, hits in score.drum_hits.items():
        shot = to_stereo(shots[inst], PAN.get(inst, 0.0))
        bus = B["kick"] if inst == "kick" else B["drums"]
        for t, v in hits:
            add_at(bus, g.sample(t), shot, MIX[inst] * v)
            if inst in REVERB_SEND:
                add_at(sends["reverb"], g.sample(t), shot, MIX[inst] * v * REVERB_SEND[inst])
    timer("drums")

    # ----------------------------------------------------------- bass ----
    for e in score.bass:
        x = ins.bass_note(e["midi"], g.sample(e["dur"]), accent=e["vel"])
        add_at(B["bass"], g.sample(e["t"]), to_stereo(x), MIX["bass"] * e["vel"])
    sub_bus = np.zeros((n, 2))
    for e in score.subs:
        add_at(sub_bus, g.sample(e["t"]), to_stereo(ins.sub_note(e["midi"], g.sample(e["dur"]))), MIX["sub"])
    timer("bass")

    # ------------------------------------------------------ pad, brass ----
    for i, e in enumerate(score.pad):
        stab = e["dur"] < g.beat
        x = ins.pad_chord(e["notes"], g.sample(e["dur"]), seed=i, attack=0.004 if stab else 0.04,
                          release=0.18 if stab else 0.35)
        add_at(B["pad"], g.sample(e["t"]), x, MIX["pad"] * e["vel"])
    B["pad"] = butter(B["pad"], "hp", 140.0, 2)
    B["pad"] = tv_biquad(B["pad"], "lp", _interp(t_axis, score.pad_cutoff, True), 1.1, block=256)
    B["pad"] = biquad(B["pad"], "hs", 2500.0, 0.7, gain_db=6.0)
    B["pad"] *= _interp(t_axis, score.pad_gain, False)[:, None]
    sends["reverb"] += B["pad"] * REVERB_SEND["pad"]
    cache = {}
    for i, e in enumerate(score.brass):
        key = (tuple(e["notes"]), g.sample(e["dur"]))
        if key not in cache:
            cache[key] = ins.brass(e["notes"], key[1], seed=len(cache))
        add_at(B["brass"], g.sample(e["t"]), cache[key], MIX["brass"] * e["vel"])
    B["brass"] = butter(B["brass"], "hp", 90.0, 2)
    sends["reverb"] += B["brass"] * REVERB_SEND["brass"]
    timer("pad+brass")

    # ----------------------------------------------------------- arps ----
    pluck_cache, cel_cache = {}, {}
    for i, e in enumerate(score.arps):
        if e["voice"] == "pluck":
            x = pluck_cache.setdefault(e["midi"], ins.pluck(e["midi"]))
            add_at(B["pluck"], g.sample(e["t"]), to_stereo(x, 0.3 if i % 2 else -0.3), MIX["pluck"] * e["vel"])
        else:
            x = cel_cache.setdefault(e["midi"], ins.celesta(e["midi"]))
            add_at(B["celesta"], g.sample(e["t"]), to_stereo(x, 0.25 if i % 2 else -0.25), MIX["celesta"] * e["vel"])
    sends["reverb"] += B["pluck"] * REVERB_SEND["pluck"]
    timer("arps")

    # --------------------------------------------------------- melody ----
    by_voice: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
    for e in score.melody:
        by_voice.setdefault(e["voice"], []).append(e)
    for voice, bus, pan in (("cry", "cry", 0.0), ("harm_a", "harm", -0.55), ("harm_b", "harm", 0.55)):
        for ph_i, ph in enumerate(_phrases(by_voice.get(voice, []))):
            y, s0 = ins.formant_cry(ph, ph[0]["onset_s"], seed=ph_i + (0 if voice == "cry" else 500 + 100 * len(voice)))
            y = y / max(np.percentile(np.abs(y), 99.7), 1e-9) * 0.8
            add_at(B[bus], s0, to_stereo(y, pan), MIX[bus if bus != "cry" else "cry"] * (1.0 if bus == "cry" else 1.0))
    timer("formant voices")
    # stutter chops: slices of the dry cry voice ("cry-cry-crying") over the next bar's held "I'm"
    dry = B["cry"].mean(axis=1)
    for a in score.adlibs:
        src = dry[g.sample(a["src_s"]): g.sample(a["src_s"] + a["src_dur_s"])].copy()
        tail = dry[g.sample(a["tail_s"]): g.sample(a["tail_s"] + a["tail_dur_s"])].copy()
        for k, t in enumerate(a["hits_s"]):
            add_at(B["chop"], g.sample(t), to_stereo(fade(src.copy(), 0.002, 0.012), (-0.6, 0.6, -0.3)[k]),
                   MIX["chop"])
        add_at(B["chop"], g.sample(a["tail_hit_s"]), to_stereo(fade(tail.copy(), 0.002, 0.02), 0.3), MIX["chop"])
    B["chop"] = butter(B["chop"], "hp", 400.0, 2)
    for voice, shift in (("saw", 0), ("saw_hi", 12)):
        prev_end, prev_m = -1.0, None
        for i, e in enumerate(by_voice.get(voice, [])):
            glide = prev_m if abs(e["onset_s"] - prev_end) < 0.02 else None
            x = ins.lead_saw(e["midi"] + shift, g.sample(e["duration_s"] * 0.92), seed=i,
                             prev_midi=None if glide is None else glide + shift)
            add_at(B["lead"], g.sample(e["onset_s"]), x, MIX[voice] * e["vel"])
            prev_end, prev_m = e["onset_s"] + e["duration_s"], e["midi"]
    for i, e in enumerate(by_voice.get("celesta", [])):
        x = cel_cache.setdefault(e["midi"], ins.celesta(e["midi"]))
        add_at(B["celesta"], g.sample(e["onset_s"]), to_stereo(x, 0.1 if i % 2 else -0.1), MIX["celesta"] * e["vel"])
    B["lead"] = biquad(butter(B["lead"], "hp", 180.0, 2), "hs", 3000.0, 0.7, gain_db=4.0)
    for k in ("cry", "harm"):
        B[k] = biquad(butter(B[k], "hp", 180.0, 2), "peak", 3500.0, 1.0, gain_db=4.0)  # presence
    leads = B["lead"] + B["cry"] + B["harm"]
    sends["reverb"] += leads * REVERB_SEND["lead"] + B["celesta"] * REVERB_SEND["celesta"] + B["chop"] * REVERB_SEND["chop"]
    sends["delay"] += leads * DELAY_SEND["lead"] + B["celesta"] * DELAY_SEND["celesta"] + B["chop"] * DELAY_SEND["chop"]
    timer("leads")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------- fx ----
    crash = ins.crash()
    for e in score.fx:
        if e["kind"] == "riser":
            x, gain = ins.riser(e["dur"], seed=int(e["t"])), MIX["riser"]
        elif e["kind"] == "revcym":
            x, gain = ins.reverse_cymbal(e["dur"], seed=int(e["t"])), MIX["revcym"]
        elif e["kind"] == "crash":
            x, gain = crash, MIX["crash"]
        elif e["kind"] == "click":
            x, gain = to_stereo(ins.camera_click(), 0.15), MIX["click"]
        else:
            raise ValueError(e["kind"])
        add_at(B["fx"], g.sample(e["t"]), x, gain * e["gain"])
    timer("fx")

    # ------------------------------------- sidechain, reverb, delay ------
    # key = the kick bus, re-normalised so the pump depth does not depend on the kick's mix level
    key = B["kick"].mean(axis=1) * np.sqrt(2) / MIX["kick"]
    gr = sidechain_gain_reduction(key, threshold_db=-14.0, ratio=4.0, attack_ms=1.5, release_ms=160.0)
    for k in ("bass", "pad", "brass", "pluck", "lead", "cry", "harm", "chop", "fx"):
        B[k] = apply_gr(B[k], gr, SIDECHAIN["lead" if k in ("cry", "harm", "chop") else k])
    B["bass"] += apply_gr(sub_bus, gr, SIDECHAIN["sub"])
    # the sleepy filter: low-pass the band (not the voice) where a section asks for it
    lp = _interp(t_axis, score.music_lp, True)
    if np.min(lp) < 18000:
        for k in BAND:
            B[k] = tv_biquad(B[k], "lp", lp, 0.8, block=256)
    timer("sidechain+lp")
    ir = make_ir()
    rev = convolve_reverb(butter(sends["reverb"], "hp", 220.0, 2), ir) * MIX["reverb"]
    B["reverb"] = apply_gr(rev, gr, SIDECHAIN["reverb"])
    dl = pingpong(sends["delay"], 0.75 * g.beat, feedback=0.42, repeats=6) * MIX["delay"]
    B["delay"] = apply_gr(dl, gr, SIDECHAIN["delay"])
    timer("reverb+delay")
    B["gr_db"] = gr
    return B


def fold_loop(x: np.ndarray, n: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Wrap everything past sample n back onto the start (seamless loop)."""
    y = x[:n].copy()
    tail = x[n:]
    while tail.shape[0]:
        k = min(n, tail.shape[0])
        y[:k] += tail[:k]
        tail = tail[k:]
    return y


def build_song_json(score: Score, n_samples: int, previews: dict, loop: bool) -> dict:
    g = Grid(score.bpm, score.sr)
    nbeats = score.total_bars * 4
    beats = [i * g.beat for i in range(nbeats)]
    melody = []
    for e in sorted(score.melody, key=lambda e: (e["onset_s"], e["voice"])):
        melody.append(dict(onset_s=e["onset_s"], duration_s=e["duration_s"], onset_sample=g.sample(e["onset_s"]),
                           note=e["note"], midi=e["midi"], freq_hz=round(440 * 2 ** ((e["midi"] - 69) / 12), 3),
                           syllable=e["syllable"], line_id=e["line_id"], lyric=e["lyric"], voice=e["voice"],
                           section=e["section"], primary=e["primary"]))
    bars = []
    for s in score.sections:
        for b in range(s["bars"]):
            ab = s["start_bar"] + b
            bars.append(dict(bar=ab, section=s["name"], bar_in_section=b, start_s=ab * g.bar,
                             chord=s["chords"][b],
                             lyric_line_ids=[l["id"] for l in score.lyric_lines if l["bar"] == ab]))
    return dict(
        title="It's 2am and I'm Crying", bpm=score.bpm, sample_rate=score.sr, beats_per_bar=4,
        beat_s=g.beat, bar_s=g.bar, key="A minor", total_bars=score.total_bars,
        music_end_s=score.music_end_s, duration_s=n_samples / score.sr, duration_samples=n_samples,
        loops_seamlessly=loop,
        time_origin="t=0 is the first sample of the WAV and the downbeat of bar 0 (bars are 0-based); "
                    "onset_sample = round(t*sr)",
        sections=score.sections,
        bars=bars,
        chords=score.chords,
        beats=beats, downbeats=beats[::4],
        kicks=[t for t, _ in score.drum_hits.get("kick", [])],
        claps=[t for t, _ in score.drum_hits.get("clap", [])],
        untz=[t for t, _ in score.drum_hits.get("untz", [])],
        sfx=[dict(kind=f["kind"], t=f["t"], section=f["section"]) for f in score.fx],
        lyric_lines=score.lyric_lines,
        adlibs=score.adlibs,
        hook_notes=[m for m in melody if m["primary"]],
        melody_events=melody,
        previews=previews,
    )


def render_song(out_dir: Path, previews: bool = True, name: str = "2am_crying",
                solo: list[str] | None = None) -> dict:
    import song

    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    T0 = time.time()
    last = [T0]

    def timer(label):
        now = time.time()
        print(f"  {label:<14} {now - last[0]:6.2f} s")
        last[0] = now

    score = expand(song.SECTIONS, song.BPM, song.SR, song.CONTOURS)
    g = Grid(score.bpm, score.sr)
    timer("expand")
    B = render_buses(score, timer)
    B.pop("gr_db")
    n_music = g.sample(score.music_end_s)
    if solo:  # audition/inspect individual buses: no master, peak-normalised, no json
        mix = sum(B[k] for k in solo)
        mix = mix / max(np.max(np.abs(mix)), 1e-9) * 0.89
        p = out_dir / f"solo_{'+'.join(solo)}.wav"
        sf.write(p, mix, SR, subtype="PCM_24")
        print(f"wrote {p}")
        return {}
    mix = sum(B.values())
    print(f"  pre-master peak {20*np.log10(np.max(np.abs(mix))):.1f} dBFS")
    if song.LOOP:
        mix = fold_loop(mix, n_music)
        P = int(3.0 * SR)  # master circularly so limiter state matches across the seam
        ext = np.concatenate([mix[-P:], mix, mix[:P]])
        y, minfo = master_chain(ext, song.TARGET_LUFS, song.CEILING_DBTP)
        y = y[P:P + n_music]
    else:
        y, minfo = master_chain(mix, song.TARGET_LUFS, song.CEILING_DBTP)
        y[-int(0.5 * SR):] *= np.linspace(1, 0, int(0.5 * SR))[:, None]
    timer("master")
    wav = out_dir / f"{name}.wav"
    sf.write(wav, y, SR, subtype="PCM_24")
    prev = {}
    if previews:
        secs = {s["name"]: s for s in score.sections}
        for key, sec in (("chorus", "chorus1"), ("bridge", "bridge")):
            st = secs[sec]["start_s"] - g.beat
            a, b = g.sample(st), g.sample(st + 10.0)
            clip = fade(y[a:b].copy(), 0.02, 0.4)
            p = out_dir / f"preview_{key}.wav"
            sf.write(p, clip, SR, subtype="PCM_24")
            prev[key] = dict(file=p.name, start_s=st, end_s=st + 10.0, section=sec)
    meta = build_song_json(score, y.shape[0], prev, song.LOOP)
    meta["master"] = minfo
    meta["render_seconds"] = round(time.time() - T0, 2)
    (out_dir / "song.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta, indent=1))
    timer("write")
    print(f"rendered {wav} ({y.shape[0]/SR:.3f} s) in {time.time() - T0:.1f} s")
    return meta
EOF

cat >> /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/synth/ITERATIONS.md <<'EOF'

## iter4: restructure to lyrics v3 "the day" (coordinator update)

Mid-task the song plan changed to the v3 timestamp litany in `LYRICS.md`:
intro 2 / chorus 8 / verse1 8 (double time) / chorus2 8 (stutter ad-libs) /
verse2 8 (double time, sleepier, silent last bar) / bridge 8 (half time, music
box, no kick) / final chorus 12 / outro 2 (= intro, seamless loop). 56 bars =
105.000 s, which replaces the earlier 104-bar 3:15 plan.

New engine pieces:
- `lyrics.py`: a lyric setter that puts every syllabified "It's [time] and I'm
  [doing]" clause on the hook rhythm (pickup on the and-of-4, time on 1, "I'm"
  on 3, verb on 4). The verb group fills the room before the next line's pickup
  (8ths, then 16ths, then 32nds, borrowing from the held "I'm" if needed). Rate
  0.5 gives double time, 2 gives half time. All lines are set globally, so
  pickups across section boundaries are honoured.
- Melodic contours per line type (cry1/2/3 escalate the CRY peak E5 -> A5 ->
  C6; "nap" descends; "poop" drops to C4; "sleep" sinks).
- 2010 electro-hop layer: synth-brass stabs (stacked saws, pitch blip, snapping
  filter envelope, saturation). They play syncopated in the verses and one big
  stab per bar in the drops.
- Vocoder-style harmonies in the final chorus: two extra formant voices on
  chord tones under the melody, panned +-0.55.
- Stutter chops in chorus 2: slices of the dry cry voice ("cry-cry-crying",
  "nap-nap-napping") retriggered on 16ths over the next bar's held "I'm".
- Verse-2 "sleepy filter": a band-wide low-pass from 16 kHz to 450 Hz across
  bars 1-7 (the voice is left out of it). Bar 8 is band-silent, with the voice a
  cappella.
- Seamless loop: the outro is the intro's material; the reverb/release tail is
  wrapped onto t=0, and the master runs on a circularly padded buffer so the
  limiter state matches across the seam. analyze.py gained a seam check.

Numbers: 105.000 s, render 10.7 s, -9.94 LUFS, TP -1.20, crest 11.0 dB, 0 clips.
Bands: sub -21.3, bass -16.5, low-mid -15.9, high-mid -26.3, air -26.7.
Sections: intro -18.2, chorus1 -9.0, verse1 -10.3, chorus2 -8.9, verse2 -11.6,
bridge -13.8, final -8.2, outro -18.3 LUFS.
Sync: 172/172 kicks, max 0.45 ms (the causal sleepy filter delays verse-2 kicks).
Seam: jump 0.009 vs a typical 99.9th-percentile step of 0.51 -> continuous.

Spectrogram: the arc reads correctly. The final chorus is the loudest. Verse 2's
top end visibly closes toward 62 s; the silent bar is a dark gap holding only the
voice's harmonics. In the chorus zoom, "CRY ing it's" sits on 4 / 4e / 4&, and the
three CRY peaks step up 659 -> 880 -> 1046 Hz as composed. In the stutter zoom
the chops show as three small arcs during the held "I'm", but faintly. In the
bridge, the sustained sub is a solid block below 120 Hz again, and the celesta
notes ring so long they read as held lines rather than music-box plinks.
Verse 1 is only 1.3 LU under the chorus.
EOF

        s=s.replace(a,b)
    open(root+f,'w').write(s)

BashGenerate chorus melody table from spec12:01 AM
input
description Generate chorus melody table from spec
uv run --project /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/synth python -c "
import sys; sys.path.insert(0,'/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/synth')
import song
from timeline import expand
s=expand(song.SECTIONS, song.BPM, song.SR, song.CONTOURS)
POS=['1','e','&','a']
def pos(st):
    st=round(st*2)/2
    if st<0: return f'(4{POS[int(st%4)] if st%1==0 else \"\"}) '.replace('4','prev 4',1) if False else ('pickup ' + {-2:'4&',-1:'4a'}.get(st, str(st)))
    b=int(st//4)+1; r=st%4
    return f'{b}{POS[int(r)] if r==int(r) else POS[int(r)]+\"+\"}'
for l in s.lyric_lines:
    if l['section']=='chorus1':
        cells=[]
        for x in l['syllables']:
            st=(x['onset_s']-l['downbeat_s'])/0.1171875
            cells.append(f\"{x['syllable']}:{x['note']}@{pos(st)}\")
        print('| '+l['text']+' | '+' '.join(cells)+' |')
"
result
